//! `mphys_core` - system ergonomics, memory lifecycle and interoperability core.
//!
//! | module          | responsibility                                                             |
//! |-----------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
//! | [`toml`]        | strict TOML subset reader used for `project.toml` and `crash_replay.toml`  |
//! | [`manifest`]    | typed, validated schema of `project.toml` (design intent only)             |
//! | [`cache`]       | content-addressed `cache/*.mphys` store, zero-copy `mmap` reader           |
//! | [`selector`]    | topology-independent boundary selector DSL + strict ambiguity contract     |
//! | [`units`]       | compile-time dimensional analysis and the 7-exponent SI wire contract      |
//! | [`arena`]       | 64-byte aligned 2 MiB page arena with O(1) reset                           |
//! | [`determinism`] | Morton/Hilbert keys, parallel exclusive scan, stable parallel radix sort   |
//! | [`flight`]      | invariant checks and the `crash_replay.toml` flight recorder               |
//! | [`mesh`]        | Hilbert-sorted SoA owner/neighbour/CellFaceCSR mesh + Gauss closure check  |
//! | [`gmsh`]        | Gmsh `.msh` 4.1 reader/writer (ASCII and binary)                           |
//! | [`cgns`]        | CGNS-style element tables (fixed, MIXED, NGON_n/NFACE_n)                   |
//! | [`openfoam`]    | OpenFOAM `polyMesh` reader/writer                                          |
//! | [`api`]         | JSON façade exported to the WebAssembly workbench                          |
//!
//! The crate has no third-party dependencies and assumes a little-endian host
//! for its zero-copy binary sections.
#![deny(unsafe_op_in_unsafe_fn)]
#[cfg(target_endian = "big")]
compile_error!("mphys_core zero-copy sections require a little-endian host");
pub mod api;
pub mod arena;
pub mod cache;
pub mod cgns;
pub mod determinism;
pub mod flight;
pub mod gmsh;
pub mod manifest;
pub mod mesh;
pub mod openfoam;
pub mod selector;
pub mod sha256;
pub mod toml;
pub mod units;

//! FIPS 180-4 SHA-256 (streaming) plus plain-old-data byte views.
//!
//! Implemented in-tree so that content addresses never depend on the version
//! of an external crate.
const K: [u32; 64] = [
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
];
/// Streaming SHA-256 hasher.
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Sha256 {
    h: [u32; 8],
    buf: [u8; 64],
    n: usize,
    len: u64,
}
impl Default for Sha256 {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}
impl Sha256 {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Sha256 {
            h: [
                0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab,
                0x5be0cd19,
            ],
            buf: [0; 64],
            n: 0,
            len: 0,
        }
    }
    pub fn update(&mut self, mut data: &[u8]) {
        self.len = self.len.wrapping_add(data.len() as u64);
        if self.n > 0 {
            let take = (64 - self.n).min(data.len());
            self.buf[self.n..self.n + take].copy_from_slice(&data[..take]);
            self.n += take;
            data = &data[take..];
            if self.n < 64 {
                return;
            }
            let block = self.buf;
            self.compress(&block);
            self.n = 0;
        }
        while data.len() >= 64 {
            let (block, rest) = data.split_at(64);
            self.compress(block.try_into().expect("64-byte block"));
            data = rest;
        }
        self.buf[..data.len()].copy_from_slice(data);
        self.n = data.len();
    }
    pub fn finalize(mut self) -> [u8; 32] {
        let bits = self.len.wrapping_mul(8);
        let mut pad = [0u8; 64];
        pad[0] = 0x80;
        let pad_len = if self.n < 56 { 56 - self.n } else { 120 - self.n };
        self.update(&pad[..pad_len]);
        self.update(&bits.to_be_bytes());
        debug_assert_eq!(self.n, 0);
        let mut out = [0u8; 32];
        for (i, w) in self.h.iter().enumerate() {
            out[4 * i..4 * i + 4].copy_from_slice(&w.to_be_bytes());
        }
        out
    }
    fn compress(&mut self, block: &[u8; 64]) {
        let mut w = [0u32; 64];
        for i in 0..16 {
            w[i] = u32::from_be_bytes([block[4 * i], block[4 * i + 1], block[4 * i + 2], block[4 * i + 3]]);
        }
        for i in 16..64 {
            let s0 = w[i - 15].rotate_right(7) ^ w[i - 15].rotate_right(18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3);
            let s1 = w[i - 2].rotate_right(17) ^ w[i - 2].rotate_right(19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10);
            w[i] = w[i - 16].wrapping_add(s0).wrapping_add(w[i - 7]).wrapping_add(s1);
        }
        let [mut a, mut b, mut c, mut d, mut e, mut f, mut g, mut h] = self.h;
        for i in 0..64 {
            let s1 = e.rotate_right(6) ^ e.rotate_right(11) ^ e.rotate_right(25);
            let ch = (e & f) ^ (!e & g);
            let t1 = h.wrapping_add(s1).wrapping_add(ch).wrapping_add(K[i]).wrapping_add(w[i]);
            let s0 = a.rotate_right(2) ^ a.rotate_right(13) ^ a.rotate_right(22);
            let maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
            let t2 = s0.wrapping_add(maj);
            h = g;
            g = f;
            f = e;
            e = d.wrapping_add(t1);
            d = c;
            c = b;
            b = a;
            a = t1.wrapping_add(t2);
        }
        for (s, v) in self.h.iter_mut().zip([a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h]) {
            *s = s.wrapping_add(v);
        }
    }
}
/// One-shot digest.
pub fn sha256(data: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32] {
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(data);
    h.finalize()
}
/// Lower-case hexadecimal encoding.
pub fn to_hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    const HEX: &[u8; 16] = b"0123456789abcdef";
    let mut s = String::with_capacity(bytes.len() * 2);
    for &b in bytes {
        s.push(HEX[(b >> 4) as usize] as char);
        s.push(HEX[(b & 15) as usize] as char);
    }
    s
}
/// Parses exactly 64 hexadecimal characters into a digest.
pub fn digest_from_hex(s: &str) -> Option<[u8; 32]> {
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    if b.len() != 64 {
        return None;
    }
    let nib = |c: u8| -> Option<u8> {
        match c {
            b'0'..=b'9' => Some(c - b'0'),
            b'a'..=b'f' => Some(c - b'a' + 10),
            b'A'..=b'F' => Some(c - b'A' + 10),
            _ => None,
        }
    };
    let mut out = [0u8; 32];
    for i in 0..32 {
        out[i] = (nib(b[2 * i])? << 4) | nib(b[2 * i + 1])?;
    }
    Some(out)
}
/// Marker for types whose in-memory representation is their wire
/// representation on little-endian hosts (no padding, every bit pattern valid).
///
/// # Safety
/// Implementors must be `Copy`, contain no padding and accept any bit pattern.
pub unsafe trait Pod: Copy + 'static {}
unsafe impl Pod for u8 {}
unsafe impl Pod for u32 {}
unsafe impl Pod for u64 {}
unsafe impl Pod for i32 {}
unsafe impl Pod for i64 {}
unsafe impl Pod for f64 {}
/// Zero-copy byte view of a POD slice.
pub fn bytes_of<T: Pod>(s: &[T]) -> &[u8] {
    // SAFETY: `T: Pod` guarantees no padding; the length covers exactly the slice.
    unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(s.as_ptr() as *const u8, std::mem::size_of_val(s)) }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn nist_vectors() {
        assert_eq!(
            to_hex(&sha256(b"")),
            "e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            to_hex(&sha256(b"abc")),
            "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            to_hex(&sha256(b"abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkijkljklmklmnlmnomnopnopq")),
            "248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1"
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn streaming_matches_one_shot_for_all_split_points() {
        let data: Vec<u8> = (0..300u32).map(|i| (i * 7 + 3) as u8).collect();
        let reference = sha256(&data);
        for split in 0..data.len() {
            let mut h = Sha256::new();
            h.update(&data[..split]);
            h.update(&data[split..]);
            assert_eq!(h.finalize(), reference, "split at {split}");
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn million_a() {
        let mut h = Sha256::new();
        let chunk = [b'a'; 1000];
        for _ in 0..1000 {
            h.update(&chunk);
        }
        assert_eq!(
            to_hex(&h.finalize()),
            "cdc76e5c9914fb9281a1c7e284d73e67f1809a48a497200e046d39ccc7112cd0"
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn hex_round_trip() {
        let d = sha256(b"mphys");
        assert_eq!(digest_from_hex(&to_hex(&d)), Some(d));
        assert_eq!(digest_from_hex("zz"), None);
    }
}

Created core/rust/src/toml.rs
Created core/rust/src/toml.rs

//! Strict TOML subset reader.
//!
//! Supported: comments, bare/quoted/dotted keys, `[table]`, `[[array.of.tables]]`,
//! basic and literal strings (single- and multi-line), integers (dec/hex/oct/bin,
//! `_` separators), floats (`inf`/`nan` included), booleans, arrays and inline
//! tables. Date-times are rejected with an explicit error - design intent has
//! no business depending on wall-clock values.
//!
//! Tables are `BTreeMap`s, so iteration order (and therefore every hash derived
//! from a sub-tree) is independent of the key order in the file.
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fmt;
pub type Table = BTreeMap<String, Value>;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Value {
    String(String),
    Integer(i64),
    Float(f64),
    Boolean(bool),
    Array(Vec<Value>),
    Table(Table),
}
impl Value {
    pub fn type_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Value::String(_) => "string",
            Value::Integer(_) => "integer",
            Value::Float(_) => "float",
            Value::Boolean(_) => "boolean",
            Value::Array(_) => "array",
            Value::Table(_) => "table",
        }
    }
    pub fn as_str(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match self {
            Value::String(s) => Some(s),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn as_i64(&self) -> Option<i64> {
        match self {
            Value::Integer(i) => Some(*i),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    /// Floats, and integers widened to `f64`.
    pub fn as_f64(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        match self {
            Value::Float(f) => Some(*f),
            Value::Integer(i) => Some(*i as f64),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn as_bool(&self) -> Option<bool> {
        match self {
            Value::Boolean(b) => Some(*b),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn as_array(&self) -> Option<&[Value]> {
        match self {
            Value::Array(a) => Some(a),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn as_table(&self) -> Option<&Table> {
        match self {
            Value::Table(t) => Some(t),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct TomlError {
    pub line: usize,
    pub col: usize,
    pub msg: String,
}
impl fmt::Display for TomlError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "line {}, column {}: {}", self.line, self.col, self.msg)
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for TomlError {}
struct Parser<'a> {
    s: &'a [u8],
    i: usize,
    line: usize,
    bol: usize,
}
/// Descends (creating intermediate tables) along `path`; arrays of tables
/// resolve to their last element, as TOML prescribes.
fn walk<'t>(root: &'t mut Table, path: &[String]) -> Result<&'t mut Table, String> {
    let mut t = root;
    for k in path {
        let v = t.entry(k.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Value::Table(Table::new()));
        t = match v {
            Value::Table(x) => x,
            Value::Array(a) => match a.last_mut() {
                Some(Value::Table(x)) => x,
                _ => return Err(format!("`{k}` is not an array of tables")),
            },
            _ => return Err(format!("key `{k}` is already defined as a plain value")),
        };
    }
    Ok(t)
}
impl<'a> Parser<'a> {
    fn error(&self, msg: impl Into<String>) -> TomlError {
        TomlError { line: self.line, col: self.i.saturating_sub(self.bol) + 1, msg: msg.into() }
    }
    fn peek(&self) -> Option<u8> {
        self.s.get(self.i).copied()
    }
    fn bump(&mut self) -> Option<u8> {
        let c = self.peek()?;
        self.i += 1;
        if c == b'\n' {
            self.line += 1;
            self.bol = self.i;
        }
        Some(c)
    }
    fn starts_with(&self, pat: &[u8]) -> bool {
        self.s[self.i..].starts_with(pat)
    }
    fn skip_ws(&mut self) {
        while matches!(self.peek(), Some(b' ' | b'\t')) {
            self.i += 1;
        }
    }
    fn skip_comment(&mut self) {
        if self.peek() == Some(b'#') {
            while let Some(c) = self.peek() {
                if c == b'\n' {
                    break;
                }
                self.i += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    /// Skips whitespace, comments and line breaks.
    fn skip_blank(&mut self) {
        loop {
            self.skip_ws();
            self.skip_comment();
            match self.peek() {
                Some(b'\n') | Some(b'\r') => {
                    self.bump();
                }
                _ => break,
            }
        }
    }
    fn expect(&mut self, c: u8) -> Result<(), TomlError> {
        if self.peek() == Some(c) {
            self.bump();
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(self.error(format!("expected `{}`", c as char)))
        }
    }
    fn simple_key(&mut self) -> Result<String, TomlError> {
        match self.peek() {
            Some(b'"') => {
                self.bump();
                self.basic_string(false)
            }
            Some(b'\'') => {
                self.bump();
                self.literal_string(false)
            }
            _ => {
                let st = self.i;
                while matches!(self.peek(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == b'_' || c == b'-') {
                    self.i += 1;
                }
                if st == self.i {
                    return Err(self.error("expected a key"));
                }
                Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&self.s[st..self.i]).into_owned())
            }
        }
    }
    fn key(&mut self) -> Result<Vec<String>, TomlError> {
        let mut path = vec![self.simple_key()?];
        loop {
            self.skip_ws();
            if self.peek() == Some(b'.') {
                self.bump();
                self.skip_ws();
                path.push(self.simple_key()?);
            } else {
                return Ok(path);
            }
        }
    }
    fn hex_escape(&mut self, digits: usize, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> Result<(), TomlError> {
        let mut v = 0u32;
        for _ in 0..digits {
            let c = self.bump().ok_or_else(|| self.error("unterminated unicode escape"))?;
            let d = (c as char).to_digit(16).ok_or_else(|| self.error("invalid unicode escape"))?;
            v = v * 16 + d;
        }
        let ch = char::from_u32(v).ok_or_else(|| self.error("escape is not a unicode scalar value"))?;
        let mut tmp = [0u8; 4];
        out.extend_from_slice(ch.encode_utf8(&mut tmp).as_bytes());
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Parses the remainder of a basic string; the opening quote(s) are consumed.
    fn basic_string(&mut self, multi: bool) -> Result<String, TomlError> {
        let mut out: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
        if multi {
            if self.peek() == Some(b'\r') {
                self.bump();
            }
            if self.peek() == Some(b'\n') {
                self.bump();
            }
        }
        loop {
            let c = self.bump().ok_or_else(|| self.error("unterminated string"))?;
            match c {
                b'"' if !multi => break,
                b'"' => {
                    if self.starts_with(b"\"\"") {
                        self.i += 2;
                        let mut extra = 0;
                        while extra < 2 && self.peek() == Some(b'"') {
                            out.push(b'"');
                            self.i += 1;
                            extra += 1;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    out.push(b'"');
                }
                b'\\' => {
                    let e = self.bump().ok_or_else(|| self.error("unterminated escape"))?;
                    match e {
                        b'b' => out.push(8),
                        b't' => out.push(b'\t'),
                        b'n' => out.push(b'\n'),
                        b'f' => out.push(12),
                        b'r' => out.push(b'\r'),
                        b'"' => out.push(b'"'),
                        b'\\' => out.push(b'\\'),
                        b'u' => self.hex_escape(4, &mut out)?,
                        b'U' => self.hex_escape(8, &mut out)?,
                        b' ' | b'\t' | b'\r' | b'\n' if multi => {
                            while matches!(self.peek(), Some(b' ' | b'\t' | b'\r' | b'\n')) {
                                self.bump();
                            }
                        }
                        _ => return Err(self.error(format!("invalid escape `\\{}`", e as char))),
                    }
                }
                b'\n' if !multi => return Err(self.error("line break inside a single-line string")),
                _ => out.push(c),
            }
        }
        String::from_utf8(out).map_err(|_| self.error("string is not valid UTF-8"))
    }
    fn literal_string(&mut self, multi: bool) -> Result<String, TomlError> {
        let mut out: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
        if multi {
            if self.peek() == Some(b'\r') {
                self.bump();
            }
            if self.peek() == Some(b'\n') {
                self.bump();
            }
        }
        loop {
            let c = self.bump().ok_or_else(|| self.error("unterminated string"))?;
            match c {
                b'\'' if !multi => break,
                b'\'' => {
                    if self.starts_with(b"''") {
                        self.i += 2;
                        let mut extra = 0;
                        while extra < 2 && self.peek() == Some(b'\'') {
                            out.push(b'\'');
                            self.i += 1;
                            extra += 1;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    out.push(b'\'');
                }
                b'\n' if !multi => return Err(self.error("line break inside a single-line string")),
                _ => out.push(c),
            }
        }
        String::from_utf8(out).map_err(|_| self.error("string is not valid UTF-8"))
    }
    fn scalar(&mut self) -> Result<Value, TomlError> {
        let st = self.i;
        while let Some(c) = self.peek() {
            if matches!(c, b',' | b']' | b'}' | b' ' | b'\t' | b'\n' | b'\r' | b'#') {
                break;
            }
            self.i += 1;
        }
        let raw = std::str::from_utf8(&self.s[st..self.i]).map_err(|_| self.error("invalid UTF-8"))?;
        let fail = |p: &Parser<'_>, msg: String| -> TomlError {
            TomlError { line: p.line, col: st.saturating_sub(p.bol) + 1, msg }
        };
        match raw {
            "" => return Err(fail(self, "expected a value".into())),
            "true" => return Ok(Value::Boolean(true)),
            "false" => return Ok(Value::Boolean(false)),
            "inf" | "+inf" => return Ok(Value::Float(f64::INFINITY)),
            "-inf" => return Ok(Value::Float(f64::NEG_INFINITY)),
            "nan" | "+nan" | "-nan" => return Ok(Value::Float(f64::NAN)),
            _ => {}
        }
        let rb = raw.as_bytes();
        if raw.contains(':') || (rb.len() >= 10 && rb[4] == b'-' && rb[7] == b'-') {
            return Err(fail(self, format!("date-time values are not supported (`{raw}`)")));
        }
        let clean: String = raw.chars().filter(|&c| c != '_').collect();
        let radix = |p: &str, r: u32| i64::from_str_radix(p, r).ok().map(Value::Integer);
        let parsed = if let Some(h) = clean.strip_prefix("0x") {
            radix(h, 16)
        } else if let Some(o) = clean.strip_prefix("0o") {
            radix(o, 8)
        } else if let Some(b) = clean.strip_prefix("0b") {
            radix(b, 2)
        } else if clean.contains(['.', 'e', 'E']) {
            let has_digit = clean.bytes().any(|c| c.is_ascii_digit());
            let well_formed = clean.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || matches!(c, b'.' | b'e' | b'E' | b'+' | b'-'));
            if has_digit && well_formed {
                clean.parse::<f64>().ok().map(Value::Float)
            } else {
                None
            }
        } else {
            clean.parse::<i64>().ok().map(Value::Integer)
        };
        parsed.ok_or_else(|| fail(self, format!("invalid value `{raw}`")))
    }
    fn value(&mut self, depth: usize) -> Result<Value, TomlError> {
        if depth > 32 {
            return Err(self.error("value nesting is too deep"));
        }
        match self.peek() {
            None => Err(self.error("expected a value")),
            Some(b'"') => {
                if self.starts_with(b"\"\"\"") {
                    self.i += 3;
                    Ok(Value::String(self.basic_string(true)?))
                } else {
                    self.bump();
                    Ok(Value::String(self.basic_string(false)?))
                }
            }
            Some(b'\'') => {
                if self.starts_with(b"'''") {
                    self.i += 3;
                    Ok(Value::String(self.literal_string(true)?))
                } else {
                    self.bump();
                    Ok(Value::String(self.literal_string(false)?))
                }
            }
            Some(b'[') => {
                self.bump();
                let mut items = Vec::new();
                loop {
                    self.skip_blank();
                    if self.peek() == Some(b']') {
                        self.bump();
                        break;
                    }
                    items.push(self.value(depth + 1)?);
                    self.skip_blank();
                    match self.peek() {
                        Some(b',') => {
                            self.bump();
                        }
                        Some(b']') => {
                            self.bump();
                            break;
                        }
                        _ => return Err(self.error("expected `,` or `]` in array")),
                    }
                }
                Ok(Value::Array(items))
            }
            Some(b'{') => {
                self.bump();
                let mut t = Table::new();
                self.skip_ws();
                if self.peek() == Some(b'}') {
                    self.bump();
                    return Ok(Value::Table(t));
                }
                loop {
                    self.skip_ws();
                    let path = self.key()?;
                    self.skip_ws();
                    self.expect(b'=')?;
                    self.skip_ws();
                    let v = self.value(depth + 1)?;
                    let (last, parent) = path.split_last().expect("key has at least one segment");
                    let tt = walk(&mut t, parent).map_err(|m| self.error(m))?;
                    if tt.insert(last.clone(), v).is_some() {
                        return Err(self.error(format!("duplicate key `{last}` in inline table")));
                    }
                    self.skip_ws();
                    match self.peek() {
                        Some(b',') => {
                            self.bump();
                        }
                        Some(b'}') => {
                            self.bump();
                            break;
                        }
                        _ => return Err(self.error("expected `,` or `}` in inline table")),
                    }
                }
                Ok(Value::Table(t))
            }
            Some(_) => self.scalar(),
        }
    }
}
/// Parses a TOML document into its root table.
pub fn parse(src: &str) -> Result<Table, TomlError> {
    let mut p = Parser { s: src.as_bytes(), i: 0, line: 1, bol: 0 };
    let mut root = Table::new();
    let mut current: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut defined: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::new();
    loop {
        p.skip_blank();
        let Some(c) = p.peek() else { break };
        if c == b'[' {
            p.bump();
            let is_array = p.peek() == Some(b'[');
            if is_array {
                p.bump();
            }
            p.skip_ws();
            let path = p.key()?;
            p.skip_ws();
            p.expect(b']')?;
            if is_array {
                p.expect(b']')?;
                let (last, parent) = path.split_last().expect("non-empty key");
                let t = walk(&mut root, parent).map_err(|m| p.error(m))?;
                match t.entry(last.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Value::Array(Vec::new())) {
                    Value::Array(a) if a.iter().all(|v| matches!(v, Value::Table(_))) => {
                        a.push(Value::Table(Table::new()));
                    }
                    _ => return Err(p.error(format!("`{last}` is not an array of tables"))),
                }
                defined.retain(|d| !d.starts_with(&path));
            } else {
                if defined.contains(&path) {
                    return Err(p.error(format!("table `[{}]` is defined twice", path.join("."))));
                }
                walk(&mut root, &path).map_err(|m| p.error(m))?;
                defined.push(path.clone());
            }
            current = path;
        } else {
            let path = p.key()?;
            p.skip_ws();
            p.expect(b'=')?;
            p.skip_ws();
            let v = p.value(0)?;
            let (last, parent) = path.split_last().expect("non-empty key");
            let mut full = current.clone();
            full.extend_from_slice(parent);
            let t = walk(&mut root, &full).map_err(|m| p.error(m))?;
            if t.contains_key(last) {
                return Err(p.error(format!("duplicate key `{last}`")));
            }
            t.insert(last.clone(), v);
        }
        p.skip_ws();
        p.skip_comment();
        match p.peek() {
            None => break,
            Some(b'\n') => {
                p.bump();
            }
            Some(b'\r') => {
                p.bump();
                if p.peek() == Some(b'\n') {
                    p.bump();
                }
            }
            Some(_) => return Err(p.error("expected end of line")),
        }
    }
    Ok(root)
}
/// Single-line basic string literal with all required escapes.
pub fn quote(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 2);
    out.push('"');
    for ch in s.chars() {
        match ch {
            '"' => out.push_str("\\\""),
            '\\' => out.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => out.push_str("\\n"),
            '\r' => out.push_str("\\r"),
            '\t' => out.push_str("\\t"),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 || c as u32 == 0x7f => out.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", c as u32)),
            c => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out.push('"');
    out
}
/// Multi-line string literal. Uses a verbatim `'''` literal whenever the text
/// allows it (so embedded manifests stay byte-identical and readable) and
/// falls back to an escaped `"""` string otherwise.
pub fn quote_multiline(s: &str) -> String {
    let verbatim_ok = !s.contains("'''")
        && !s.ends_with('\'')
        && s.chars().all(|c| c == '\n' || c == '\t' || (c as u32) >= 0x20 && c as u32 != 0x7f);
    if verbatim_ok {
        return format!("'''\n{s}'''");
    }
    let mut out = String::from("\"\"\"\n");
    for ch in s.chars() {
        match ch {
            '"' => out.push_str("\\\""),
            '\\' => out.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => out.push('\n'),
            '\t' => out.push('\t'),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 || c as u32 == 0x7f => out.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", c as u32)),
            c => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out.push_str("\"\"\"");
    out
}
/// TOML float literal that round-trips exactly (`nan`/`inf` included).
pub fn float(v: f64) -> String {
    if v.is_nan() {
        "nan".into()
    } else if v.is_infinite() {
        if v > 0.0 { "inf".into() } else { "-inf".into() }
    } else {
        format!("{v:?}")
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn get<'a>(t: &'a Table, path: &[&str]) -> &'a Value {
        let mut v = t.get(path[0]).unwrap();
        for k in &path[1..] {
            v = v.as_table().unwrap().get(*k).unwrap();
        }
        v
    }
    #[test]
    fn scalars_tables_and_arrays() {
        let t = parse(
            r#"
            title = "a \"quoted\" \u00e9" # trailing comment
            lit = 'C:\raw\path'
            n = 1_000
            hex = 0xff
            x = -1.5e-3
            flag = true
            big = inf
            v = [1, 2.5,
                 3,   # comment inside array
            ]
            pt = { x = 1, y = { z = "deep" } }
            a.b.c = 7
            [solver]
            cfl = 50.0
            [solver.linear]
            tol = 1e-8
            "#,
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["title"]).as_str(), Some("a \"quoted\" é"));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["lit"]).as_str(), Some("C:\\raw\\path"));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["n"]).as_i64(), Some(1000));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["hex"]).as_i64(), Some(255));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["x"]).as_f64(), Some(-1.5e-3));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["flag"]).as_bool(), Some(true));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["big"]).as_f64(), Some(f64::INFINITY));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["v"]).as_array().unwrap().len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["pt", "y", "z"]).as_str(), Some("deep"));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["a", "b", "c"]).as_i64(), Some(7));
        assert_eq!(get(&t, &["solver", "linear", "tol"]).as_f64(), Some(1e-8));
    }
    #[test]
    fn arrays_of_tables_with_subtables() {
        let t = parse("[[bc]]\nname = \"in\"\n[bc.values]\np = 1\n[[bc]]\nname = \"out\"\n[bc.values]\np = 2\n").unwrap();
        let a = t["bc"].as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(a[1].as_table().unwrap()["values"].as_table().unwrap()["p"].as_i64(), Some(2));
    }
    #[test]
    fn multiline_strings() {
        let t = parse("a = \"\"\"\nline1\nline2 \\\n   joined\"\"\"\nb = '''\nraw \\n 'x'\n'''\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t["a"].as_str(), Some("line1\nline2 joined"));
        assert_eq!(t["b"].as_str(), Some("raw \\n 'x'\n"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn errors_carry_positions() {
        let e = parse("a = 1\na = 2\n").unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.line, 2);
        assert!(e.msg.contains("duplicate"));
        let e = parse("[t]\nx = 1\n[t]\n").unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.line, 3);
        let e = parse("when = 2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\n").unwrap_err();
        assert!(e.msg.contains("date-time"));
        assert!(parse("x = 1 y = 2\n").is_err());
        assert!(parse("x = \"unterminated\n").is_err());
        assert!(parse("x = 1.2.3\n").is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn writer_helpers_round_trip() {
        for text in ["plain", "with \"quotes\" and \\ slash\n and newline", "ends with '", "has ''' inside\n"] {
            let doc = format!("a = {}\nb = {}\n", quote(text), quote_multiline(text));
            let t = parse(&doc).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(t["a"].as_str(), Some(text), "{doc}");
            assert_eq!(t["b"].as_str(), Some(text), "{doc}");
        }
        let t = parse(&format!("a = {}\nb = {}\nc = {}\n", float(1e-7), float(f64::NAN), float(3.0))).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t["a"].as_f64(), Some(1e-7));
        assert!(t["b"].as_f64().unwrap().is_nan());
        assert_eq!(t["c"], Value::Float(3.0));
    }
}

Created core/rust/src/units.rs
Created core/rust/src/units.rs

//! Compile-time dimensional analysis and the 7-exponent SI wire contract.
//!
//! A [`Quantity<D>`] is a `#[repr(transparent)]` `f64` whose dimension `D`
//! carries seven type-level integers (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd). Multiplying and
//! dividing quantities adds/subtracts those integers *in the type system*; adding
//! quantities of different dimensions does not compile:
//!
//! ```compile_fail
//! use mphys_core::units::*;
//! let _ = Length::new(1.0) + Pressure::new(101325.0); // Length + Pressure
//! ```
//!
//! Pressures that mean different things are different types. The ideal-gas law
//! accepts only an absolute pressure:
//!
//! ```compile_fail
//! use mphys_core::units::*;
//! let p = GaugePressurePa::new(0.0);
//! let _ = ideal_gas_density(p, SpecificGasConstant::new(287.05), Temperature::new(300.0));
//! ```
//!
//! and a y+ target is not a length the mesher can consume:
//!
//! ```compile_fail
//! use mphys_core::units::*;
//! fn mesher(_dy1: FirstLayerThicknessM) {}
//! mesher(TargetYPlus::new(1.0).unwrap());
//! ```
use core::cmp::Ordering;
use core::fmt;
use core::marker::PhantomData;
use core::ops::{Add, AddAssign, Div, Mul, Neg, Sub, SubAssign};
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Type-level integers (normalised Peano form: Z, S<S<..Z>>, P<P<..Z>>)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Type-level integer.
pub trait Int {
    const VALUE: i8;
    type Succ: Int;
    type Pred: Int;
    type Opp: Int;
}
pub trait NonNeg: Int {}
pub trait NonPos: Int {}
/// Zero.
pub struct Z;
/// Successor of a non-negative integer.
pub struct S<N>(PhantomData<N>);
/// Predecessor of a non-positive integer.
pub struct P<N>(PhantomData<N>);
impl Int for Z {
    const VALUE: i8 = 0;
    type Succ = S<Z>;
    type Pred = P<Z>;
    type Opp = Z;
}
impl NonNeg for Z {}
impl NonPos for Z {}
impl<N: NonNeg> Int for S<N> {
    const VALUE: i8 = N::VALUE + 1;
    type Succ = S<S<N>>;
    type Pred = N;
    type Opp = <N::Opp as Int>::Pred;
}
impl<N: NonNeg> NonNeg for S<N> {}
impl<N: NonPos> Int for P<N> {
    const VALUE: i8 = N::VALUE - 1;
    type Succ = N;
    type Pred = P<P<N>>;
    type Opp = <N::Opp as Int>::Succ;
}
impl<N: NonPos> NonPos for P<N> {}
/// Type-level addition.
pub trait IntAdd<R: Int>: Int {
    type Out: Int;
}
impl<R: Int> IntAdd<R> for Z {
    type Out = R;
}
impl<N: NonNeg + IntAdd<R>, R: Int> IntAdd<R> for S<N> {
    type Out = <<N as IntAdd<R>>::Out as Int>::Succ;
}
impl<N: NonPos + IntAdd<R>, R: Int> IntAdd<R> for P<N> {
    type Out = <<N as IntAdd<R>>::Out as Int>::Pred;
}
/// Type-level subtraction.
pub trait IntSub<R: Int>: Int {
    type Out: Int;
}
impl<L, R> IntSub<R> for L
where
    R: Int,
    L: Int + IntAdd<<R as Int>::Opp>,
{
    type Out = <L as IntAdd<<R as Int>::Opp>>::Out;
}
pub type P1 = S<Z>;
pub type P2 = S<P1>;
pub type P3 = S<P2>;
pub type P4 = S<P3>;
pub type N1 = P<Z>;
pub type N2 = P<N1>;
pub type N3 = P<N2>;
pub type N4 = P<N3>;
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dimensions and quantities
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// A physical dimension: exponents of (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd).
pub trait Dim {
    const EXP: [i8; 7];
}
/// Dimension built from seven type-level integers.
pub struct Dims<M, L, T, Th, N, I, J>(PhantomData<(M, L, T, Th, N, I, J)>);
impl<M: Int, L: Int, T: Int, Th: Int, N: Int, I: Int, J: Int> Dim for Dims<M, L, T, Th, N, I, J> {
    const EXP: [i8; 7] = [M::VALUE, L::VALUE, T::VALUE, Th::VALUE, N::VALUE, I::VALUE, J::VALUE];
}
pub trait DimMul<R: Dim>: Dim {
    type Out: Dim;
}
pub trait DimDiv<R: Dim>: Dim {
    type Out: Dim;
}
impl<M1, L1, T1, Th1, N1_, I1, J1, M2, L2, T2, Th2, N2_, I2, J2> DimMul<Dims<M2, L2, T2, Th2, N2_, I2, J2>>
    for Dims<M1, L1, T1, Th1, N1_, I1, J1>
where
    M1: IntAdd<M2>,
    L1: IntAdd<L2>,
    T1: IntAdd<T2>,
    Th1: IntAdd<Th2>,
    N1_: IntAdd<N2_>,
    I1: IntAdd<I2>,
    J1: IntAdd<J2>,
    M2: Int,
    L2: Int,
    T2: Int,
    Th2: Int,
    N2_: Int,
    I2: Int,
    J2: Int,
{
    type Out = Dims<
        <M1 as IntAdd<M2>>::Out,
        <L1 as IntAdd<L2>>::Out,
        <T1 as IntAdd<T2>>::Out,
        <Th1 as IntAdd<Th2>>::Out,
        <N1_ as IntAdd<N2_>>::Out,
        <I1 as IntAdd<I2>>::Out,
        <J1 as IntAdd<J2>>::Out,
    >;
}
impl<M1, L1, T1, Th1, N1_, I1, J1, M2, L2, T2, Th2, N2_, I2, J2> DimDiv<Dims<M2, L2, T2, Th2, N2_, I2, J2>>
    for Dims<M1, L1, T1, Th1, N1_, I1, J1>
where
    M1: IntSub<M2>,
    L1: IntSub<L2>,
    T1: IntSub<T2>,
    Th1: IntSub<Th2>,
    N1_: IntSub<N2_>,
    I1: IntSub<I2>,
    J1: IntSub<J2>,
    M2: Int,
    L2: Int,
    T2: Int,
    Th2: Int,
    N2_: Int,
    I2: Int,
    J2: Int,
{
    type Out = Dims<
        <M1 as IntSub<M2>>::Out,
        <L1 as IntSub<L2>>::Out,
        <T1 as IntSub<T2>>::Out,
        <Th1 as IntSub<Th2>>::Out,
        <N1_ as IntSub<N2_>>::Out,
        <I1 as IntSub<I2>>::Out,
        <J1 as IntSub<J2>>::Out,
    >;
}
/// A value in coherent SI units whose dimension is tracked by the type system.
/// Layout-identical to `f64` (`#[repr(transparent)]`): zero runtime overhead.
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct Quantity<D: Dim>(f64, PhantomData<fn() -> D>);
impl<D: Dim> Quantity<D> {
    /// Exponents of (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd).
    pub const DIM: [i8; 7] = D::EXP;
    /// Wraps a value expressed in coherent SI units.
    pub const fn new(si_value: f64) -> Self {
        Quantity(si_value, PhantomData)
    }
    /// The value in coherent SI units.
    pub const fn value(self) -> f64 {
        self.0
    }
    pub fn abs(self) -> Self {
        Self::new(self.0.abs())
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0, dim: D::EXP, kind: wire_kind::PLAIN }
    }
    /// Decodes a wire value, rejecting any dimension mismatch.
    pub fn from_wire(w: SiWire) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        if w.dim != D::EXP {
            return Err(UnitError::WireDimensionMismatch { expected: D::EXP, got: w.dim });
        }
        Ok(Self::new(w.value))
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Clone for Quantity<D> {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        *self
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Copy for Quantity<D> {}
impl<D: Dim> PartialEq for Quantity<D> {
    fn eq(&self, o: &Self) -> bool {
        self.0 == o.0
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> PartialOrd for Quantity<D> {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, o: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> {
        self.0.partial_cmp(&o.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Default for Quantity<D> {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(0.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> fmt::Debug for Quantity<D> {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{} [{}]", self.0, dim_string(D::EXP))
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Add for Quantity<D> {
    type Output = Self;
    fn add(self, o: Self) -> Self {
        Self::new(self.0 + o.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Sub for Quantity<D> {
    type Output = Self;
    fn sub(self, o: Self) -> Self {
        Self::new(self.0 - o.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> AddAssign for Quantity<D> {
    fn add_assign(&mut self, o: Self) {
        self.0 += o.0;
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> SubAssign for Quantity<D> {
    fn sub_assign(&mut self, o: Self) {
        self.0 -= o.0;
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Neg for Quantity<D> {
    type Output = Self;
    fn neg(self) -> Self {
        Self::new(-self.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Mul<f64> for Quantity<D> {
    type Output = Self;
    fn mul(self, k: f64) -> Self {
        Self::new(self.0 * k)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Mul<Quantity<D>> for f64 {
    type Output = Quantity<D>;
    fn mul(self, q: Quantity<D>) -> Quantity<D> {
        Quantity::new(self * q.0)
    }
}
impl<D: Dim> Div<f64> for Quantity<D> {
    type Output = Self;
    fn div(self, k: f64) -> Self {
        Self::new(self.0 / k)
    }
}
impl<A: Dim + DimMul<B>, B: Dim> Mul<Quantity<B>> for Quantity<A> {
    type Output = Quantity<<A as DimMul<B>>::Out>;
    fn mul(self, o: Quantity<B>) -> Self::Output {
        Quantity::new(self.0 * o.0)
    }
}
impl<A: Dim + DimDiv<B>, B: Dim> Div<Quantity<B>> for Quantity<A> {
    type Output = Quantity<<A as DimDiv<B>>::Out>;
    fn div(self, o: Quantity<B>) -> Self::Output {
        Quantity::new(self.0 / o.0)
    }
}
macro_rules! quantity_alias {
    ($( $(#[$meta:meta])* $name:ident = [$m:ty, $l:ty, $t:ty, $th:ty, $n:ty, $i:ty, $j:ty]; )*) => {
        $( $(#[$meta])* pub type $name = Quantity<Dims<$m, $l, $t, $th, $n, $i, $j>>; )*
    };
}
quantity_alias! {
    Dimensionless = [Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// kg
    Mass = [P1, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// m
    Length = [Z, P1, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// s
    Time = [Z, Z, P1, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// K
    Temperature = [Z, Z, Z, P1, Z, Z, Z];
    /// mol
    Amount = [Z, Z, Z, Z, P1, Z, Z];
    /// A
    Current = [Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, P1, Z];
    /// cd
    LuminousIntensity = [Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z, P1];
    /// m^2
    Area = [Z, P2, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// m^3
    Volume = [Z, P3, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// m/s
    Velocity = [Z, P1, N1, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// m/s^2
    Acceleration = [Z, P1, N2, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// kg/m^3
    Density = [P1, N3, Z, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// kg/s
    MassFlow = [P1, Z, N1, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// Pa = kg/(m s^2)
    Pressure = [P1, N1, N2, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// N = kg m/s^2
    Force = [P1, P1, N2, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// Pa s = kg/(m s)
    DynamicViscosity = [P1, N1, N1, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// m^2/s
    KinematicViscosity = [Z, P2, N1, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// J = kg m^2/s^2
    Energy = [P1, P2, N2, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// W = kg m^2/s^3
    Power = [P1, P2, N3, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// W/m^2 = kg/s^3
    HeatFlux = [P1, Z, N3, Z, Z, Z, Z];
    /// J/(kg K) = m^2/(s^2 K)
    SpecificGasConstant = [Z, P2, N2, N1, Z, Z, Z];
    /// W/(m K) = kg m/(s^3 K)
    ThermalConductivity = [P1, P1, N3, N1, Z, Z, Z];
}
impl From<Dimensionless> for f64 {
    fn from(q: Dimensionless) -> f64 {
        q.value()
    }
}
/// Human-readable SI unit string for an exponent vector, e.g. `kg m^-1 s^-2`.
pub fn dim_string(exp: [i8; 7]) -> String {
    const SYM: [&str; 7] = ["kg", "m", "s", "K", "mol", "A", "cd"];
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    for (e, s) in exp.iter().zip(SYM) {
        match *e {
            0 => {}
            1 => parts.push(s.to_string()),
            e => parts.push(format!("{s}^{e}")),
        }
    }
    if parts.is_empty() { "1".to_string() } else { parts.join(" ") }
}
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Errors
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum UnitError {
    /// Absolute pressure must satisfy p > 0 Pa.
    NonPositiveAbsolutePressure(f64),
    NonFinite(&'static str),
    NonPositive(&'static str, f64),
    WireDimensionMismatch { expected: [i8; 7], got: [i8; 7] },
    WireKindMismatch { expected: u8, got: u8 },
    UnknownUnit(String),
}
impl fmt::Display for UnitError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            UnitError::NonPositiveAbsolutePressure(p) => {
                write!(f, "absolute pressure must be > 0 Pa (got {p} Pa); use gauge_pa or p_rgh_pa for relative pressures")
            }
            UnitError::NonFinite(what) => write!(f, "{what} must be finite"),
            UnitError::NonPositive(what, v) => write!(f, "{what} must be > 0 (got {v})"),
            UnitError::WireDimensionMismatch { expected, got } => write!(
                f,
                "SI wire dimension mismatch: expected [{}], got [{}]",
                dim_string(*expected),
                dim_string(*got)
            ),
            UnitError::WireKindMismatch { expected, got } => {
                write!(f, "SI wire semantic kind mismatch: expected {expected}, got {got}")
            }
            UnitError::UnknownUnit(u) => write!(f, "unknown length unit `{u}` (expected m, cm, mm, um, in or ft)"),
        }
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for UnitError {}
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 7-element SI wire contract
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Semantic tags carried next to the exponents on the wire.
pub mod wire_kind {
    pub const PLAIN: u8 = 0;
    pub const ABSOLUTE_PRESSURE: u8 = 1;
    pub const GAUGE_PRESSURE: u8 = 2;
    pub const REDUCED_PRESSURE_P_RGH: u8 = 3;
    pub const FIRST_LAYER_THICKNESS: u8 = 4;
    pub const TARGET_Y_PLUS: u8 = 5;
}
/// 16-byte wire record: an SI value, its seven base-unit exponents
/// (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd) and a semantic kind tag. Identical layout in the
/// C++ core (`mphys::SiWire`).
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct SiWire {
    pub value: f64,
    pub dim: [i8; 7],
    pub kind: u8,
}
const _: () = assert!(core::mem::size_of::<SiWire>() == 16 && core::mem::align_of::<SiWire>() == 8);
impl SiWire {
    pub fn to_le_bytes(self) -> [u8; 16] {
        let mut b = [0u8; 16];
        b[..8].copy_from_slice(&self.value.to_le_bytes());
        for i in 0..7 {
            b[8 + i] = self.dim[i] as u8;
        }
        b[15] = self.kind;
        b
    }
    pub fn from_le_bytes(b: [u8; 16]) -> Self {
        let mut dim = [0i8; 7];
        for i in 0..7 {
            dim[i] = b[8 + i] as i8;
        }
        SiWire { value: f64::from_le_bytes(b[..8].try_into().expect("8 bytes")), dim, kind: b[15] }
    }
    fn expect(self, dim: [i8; 7], kind: u8) -> Result<f64, UnitError> {
        if self.dim != dim {
            return Err(UnitError::WireDimensionMismatch { expected: dim, got: self.dim });
        }
        if self.kind != kind {
            return Err(UnitError::WireKindMismatch { expected: kind, got: self.kind });
        }
        Ok(self.value)
    }
}
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pressures that must never be confused
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Thermodynamic (absolute) pressure. Invariant: finite and `p > 0 Pa`.
/// The only pressure accepted by compressible / ideal-gas / Arrhenius laws.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
pub struct AbsolutePressurePa(Pressure);
impl AbsolutePressurePa {
    pub fn new(pa: f64) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        if !pa.is_finite() {
            return Err(UnitError::NonFinite("absolute pressure"));
        }
        if pa <= 0.0 {
            return Err(UnitError::NonPositiveAbsolutePressure(pa));
        }
        Ok(AbsolutePressurePa(Pressure::new(pa)))
    }
    pub fn get(self) -> Pressure {
        self.0
    }
    pub fn to_gauge(self, reference: AbsolutePressurePa) -> GaugePressurePa {
        GaugePressurePa(self.0 - reference.0)
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0.value(), dim: Pressure::DIM, kind: wire_kind::ABSOLUTE_PRESSURE }
    }
    pub fn from_wire(w: SiWire) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        Self::new(w.expect(Pressure::DIM, wire_kind::ABSOLUTE_PRESSURE)?)
    }
}
/// Pressure relative to an explicit reference pressure. May be negative.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
pub struct GaugePressurePa(Pressure);
impl GaugePressurePa {
    pub fn new(pa: f64) -> Self {
        GaugePressurePa(Pressure::new(pa))
    }
    pub fn get(self) -> Pressure {
        self.0
    }
    /// The only way to obtain an absolute pressure: name the reference.
    pub fn to_absolute(self, reference: AbsolutePressurePa) -> Result<AbsolutePressurePa, UnitError> {
        AbsolutePressurePa::new((self.0 + reference.0).value())
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0.value(), dim: Pressure::DIM, kind: wire_kind::GAUGE_PRESSURE }
    }
    pub fn from_wire(w: SiWire) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        Ok(Self::new(w.expect(Pressure::DIM, wire_kind::GAUGE_PRESSURE)?))
    }
}
/// Reduced pressure `p_rgh = p - rho g.x` used by buoyant solvers.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
pub struct ReducedPressurePRgh(Pressure);
impl ReducedPressurePRgh {
    pub fn new(pa: f64) -> Self {
        ReducedPressurePRgh(Pressure::new(pa))
    }
    pub fn get(self) -> Pressure {
        self.0
    }
    fn hydrostatic(rho: Density, g: [Acceleration; 3], x: [Length; 3]) -> Pressure {
        rho * (g[0] * x[0] + g[1] * x[1] + g[2] * x[2])
    }
    pub fn from_absolute(p: AbsolutePressurePa, rho: Density, g: [Acceleration; 3], x: [Length; 3]) -> Self {
        ReducedPressurePRgh(p.get() - Self::hydrostatic(rho, g, x))
    }
    pub fn to_absolute(
        self,
        rho: Density,
        g: [Acceleration; 3],
        x: [Length; 3],
    ) -> Result<AbsolutePressurePa, UnitError> {
        AbsolutePressurePa::new((self.0 + Self::hydrostatic(rho, g, x)).value())
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0.value(), dim: Pressure::DIM, kind: wire_kind::REDUCED_PRESSURE_P_RGH }
    }
    pub fn from_wire(w: SiWire) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        Ok(Self::new(w.expect(Pressure::DIM, wire_kind::REDUCED_PRESSURE_P_RGH)?))
    }
}
/// Ideal-gas density `rho = p / (R T)`. Only an absolute pressure type-checks.
pub fn ideal_gas_density(p: AbsolutePressurePa, r: SpecificGasConstant, t: Temperature) -> Density {
    p.get() / (r * t)
}
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// First-layer thickness vs. y+ target
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Wall-normal height of the first prism layer in metres - what the mesher needs.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
pub struct FirstLayerThicknessM(Length);
impl FirstLayerThicknessM {
    pub fn new(m: f64) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        if !m.is_finite() {
            return Err(UnitError::NonFinite("first layer thickness"));
        }
        if m <= 0.0 {
            return Err(UnitError::NonPositive("first layer thickness", m));
        }
        Ok(FirstLayerThicknessM(Length::new(m)))
    }
    pub fn get(self) -> Length {
        self.0
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0.value(), dim: Length::DIM, kind: wire_kind::FIRST_LAYER_THICKNESS }
    }
    pub fn from_wire(w: SiWire) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        Self::new(w.expect(Length::DIM, wire_kind::FIRST_LAYER_THICKNESS)?)
    }
}
/// Reference state needed to turn a y+ target into a length.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct FlowReference {
    pub u_inf: Velocity,
    pub rho: Density,
    pub mu: DynamicViscosity,
    pub l_ref: Length,
}
/// Intermediate values of the y+ estimate, kept for reporting.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct YPlusEstimate {
    pub reynolds: f64,
    pub skin_friction: f64,
    pub wall_shear: Pressure,
    pub friction_velocity: Velocity,
    pub first_layer: FirstLayerThicknessM,
}
/// Dimensionless wall distance target. It is an *estimate request*, not a
/// length: it can only become a [`FirstLayerThicknessM`] through
/// [`TargetYPlus::resolve`] with an explicit `(U_inf, rho, mu, L_ref)`.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
pub struct TargetYPlus(f64);
impl TargetYPlus {
    pub fn new(y_plus: f64) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        if !y_plus.is_finite() {
            return Err(UnitError::NonFinite("target y+"));
        }
        if y_plus <= 0.0 {
            return Err(UnitError::NonPositive("target y+", y_plus));
        }
        Ok(TargetYPlus(y_plus))
    }
    pub fn get(self) -> f64 {
        self.0
    }
    pub fn to_wire(self) -> SiWire {
        SiWire { value: self.0, dim: Dimensionless::DIM, kind: wire_kind::TARGET_Y_PLUS }
    }
    /// Flat-plate turbulent estimate: `Cf = 0.058 Re_L^-0.2`,
    /// `tau_w = Cf rho U^2 / 2`, `u_tau = sqrt(tau_w / rho)`,
    /// `dy1 = y+ mu / (rho u_tau)` (wall spacing convention).
    pub fn resolve(self, r: &FlowReference) -> Result<YPlusEstimate, UnitError> {
        for (name, v) in [
            ("reference velocity", r.u_inf.value()),
            ("reference density", r.rho.value()),
            ("reference viscosity", r.mu.value()),
            ("reference length", r.l_ref.value()),
        ] {
            if !v.is_finite() {
                return Err(UnitError::NonFinite(name));
            }
            if v <= 0.0 {
                return Err(UnitError::NonPositive(name, v));
            }
        }
        // Every line below is dimension-checked by the compiler.
        let re: Dimensionless = r.rho * r.u_inf * r.l_ref / r.mu;
        let cf = 0.058 * re.value().powf(-0.2);
        let tau_w: Pressure = r.rho * r.u_inf * r.u_inf * (0.5 * cf);
        let u_tau = Velocity::new((tau_w / r.rho).value().sqrt());
        let dy1: Length = r.mu / (r.rho * u_tau) * self.0;
        Ok(YPlusEstimate {
            reynolds: re.value(),
            skin_friction: cf,
            wall_shear: tau_w,
            friction_velocity: u_tau,
            first_layer: FirstLayerThicknessM::new(dy1.value())?,
        })
    }
}
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Source length units
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Length unit of the CAD source file. Converted to metres exactly once, at import.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum LengthUnit {
    M,
    Cm,
    Mm,
    Um,
    In,
    Ft,
}
impl LengthUnit {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, UnitError> {
        Ok(match s {
            "m" => LengthUnit::M,
            "cm" => LengthUnit::Cm,
            "mm" => LengthUnit::Mm,
            "um" => LengthUnit::Um,
            "in" => LengthUnit::In,
            "ft" => LengthUnit::Ft,
            other => return Err(UnitError::UnknownUnit(other.to_string())),
        })
    }
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            LengthUnit::M => "m",
            LengthUnit::Cm => "cm",
            LengthUnit::Mm => "mm",
            LengthUnit::Um => "um",
            LengthUnit::In => "in",
            LengthUnit::Ft => "ft",
        }
    }
    /// Metres per source unit.
    pub fn metres_per_unit(self) -> f64 {
        match self {
            LengthUnit::M => 1.0,
            LengthUnit::Cm => 1.0e-2,
            LengthUnit::Mm => 1.0e-3,
            LengthUnit::Um => 1.0e-6,
            LengthUnit::In => 0.0254,
            LengthUnit::Ft => 0.3048,
        }
    }
    pub fn length(self, value_in_unit: f64) -> Length {
        Length::new(value_in_unit * self.metres_per_unit())
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn type_level_integers() {
        assert_eq!(<P3 as Int>::VALUE, 3);
        assert_eq!(<N4 as Int>::VALUE, -4);
        assert_eq!(<<P2 as IntAdd<N3>>::Out as Int>::VALUE, -1);
        assert_eq!(<<N2 as IntAdd<P2>>::Out as Int>::VALUE, 0);
        assert_eq!(<<N1 as IntSub<N3>>::Out as Int>::VALUE, 2);
        assert_eq!(<<P1 as IntSub<P4>>::Out as Int>::VALUE, -3);
    }
    #[test]
    fn derived_dimensions_are_computed_by_the_compiler() {
        let l = Length::new(2.0);
        let area: Area = l * l;
        assert_eq!(area.value(), 4.0);
        let mdot = MassFlow::new(6.0);
        let rho = Density::new(1.5);
        let v: Velocity = mdot / (rho * area);
        assert_eq!(v.value(), 1.0);
        let f: Force = Pressure::new(10.0) * area;
        assert_eq!(f.value(), 40.0);
        let nu: KinematicViscosity = DynamicViscosity::new(1.8e-5) / rho;
        assert!((nu.value() - 1.2e-5).abs() < 1e-18);
        let re: Dimensionless = v * l / nu;
        assert!((f64::from(re) - 2.0 / 1.2e-5).abs() < 1e-6);
        assert_eq!(Pressure::DIM, [1, -1, -2, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(SpecificGasConstant::DIM, [0, 2, -2, -1, 0, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(dim_string(Pressure::DIM), "kg m^-1 s^-2");
        assert_eq!(core::mem::size_of::<Pressure>(), 8);
    }
    #[test]
    fn absolute_pressure_invariant() {
        assert!(AbsolutePressurePa::new(101325.0).is_ok());
        assert_eq!(AbsolutePressurePa::new(0.0), Err(UnitError::NonPositiveAbsolutePressure(0.0)));
        assert!(AbsolutePressurePa::new(-5.0).is_err());
        assert!(AbsolutePressurePa::new(f64::NAN).is_err());
        let p_ref = AbsolutePressurePa::new(101325.0).unwrap();
        let g = GaugePressurePa::new(-2000.0);
        let p = g.to_absolute(p_ref).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(p.get().value(), 99325.0);
        assert_eq!(p.to_gauge(p_ref), g);
        assert!(GaugePressurePa::new(-2.0e5).to_absolute(p_ref).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn p_rgh_round_trip() {
        let rho = Density::new(998.0);
        let g = [Acceleration::new(0.0), Acceleration::new(-9.81), Acceleration::new(0.0)];
        let x = [Length::new(0.3), Length::new(2.0), Length::new(0.0)];
        let p = AbsolutePressurePa::new(120000.0).unwrap();
        let prgh = ReducedPressurePRgh::from_absolute(p, rho, g, x);
        assert!((prgh.get().value() - (120000.0 + 998.0 * 9.81 * 2.0)).abs() < 1e-9);
        let back = prgh.to_absolute(rho, g, x).unwrap();
        assert!((back.get().value() - 120000.0).abs() < 1e-9);
    }
    #[test]
    fn ideal_gas_law_is_dimensionally_closed() {
        let rho = ideal_gas_density(
            AbsolutePressurePa::new(101325.0).unwrap(),
            SpecificGasConstant::new(287.05),
            Temperature::new(288.15),
        );
        assert!((rho.value() - 1.2250).abs() < 1e-3);
    }
    #[test]
    fn y_plus_resolves_to_a_length_only_with_a_reference() {
        let r = FlowReference {
            u_inf: Velocity::new(50.0),
            rho: Density::new(1.225),
            mu: DynamicViscosity::new(1.8e-5),
            l_ref: Length::new(1.0),
        };
        let est = TargetYPlus::new(1.0).unwrap().resolve(&r).unwrap();
        let dy = est.first_layer.get().value();
        assert!(dy > 7.0e-6 && dy < 8.5e-6, "dy1 = {dy}");
        // Self-consistency: recomputing y+ from the result returns the target.
        let y_plus = r.rho.value() * est.friction_velocity.value() * dy / r.mu.value();
        assert!((y_plus - 1.0).abs() < 1e-12);
        let est30 = TargetYPlus::new(30.0).unwrap().resolve(&r).unwrap();
        assert!((est30.first_layer.get().value() / dy - 30.0).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!(TargetYPlus::new(0.0).is_err());
        let bad = FlowReference { u_inf: Velocity::new(0.0), ..r };
        assert!(TargetYPlus::new(1.0).unwrap().resolve(&bad).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn wire_contract() {
        let w = Pressure::new(250.0).to_wire();
        let bytes = w.to_le_bytes();
        assert_eq!(bytes.len(), 16);
        assert_eq!(SiWire::from_le_bytes(bytes), w);
        assert_eq!(Pressure::from_wire(w).unwrap().value(), 250.0);
        assert!(matches!(Length::from_wire(w), Err(UnitError::WireDimensionMismatch { .. })));
        let abs = AbsolutePressurePa::new(101325.0).unwrap().to_wire();
        assert!(AbsolutePressurePa::from_wire(abs).is_ok());
        // A gauge pressure on the wire can never be decoded as absolute.
        let gauge = GaugePressurePa::new(0.0).to_wire();
        assert!(matches!(AbsolutePressurePa::from_wire(gauge), Err(UnitError::WireKindMismatch { .. })));
        // Nor a y+ target as a first-layer thickness.
        let yp = TargetYPlus::new(1.0).unwrap().to_wire();
        assert!(FirstLayerThicknessM::from_wire(yp).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn length_units() {
        assert_eq!(LengthUnit::parse("mm").unwrap().length(250.0).value(), 0.25);
        assert_eq!(LengthUnit::parse("in").unwrap().length(1.0).value(), 0.0254);
        assert!(LengthUnit::parse("furlong").is_err());
    }
}
