    using rep = Rep;
    /// Exponents of (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd).
    static constexpr std::array<std::int8_t, 7> dim{Kg, M, S, K, Mol, A, Cd};
    constexpr Quantity() noexcept = default;
    constexpr explicit Quantity(Rep si_value) noexcept : v_(si_value) {}
    /// Value in coherent SI units.
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Rep value() const noexcept { return v_; }
    constexpr Quantity& operator+=(Quantity o) noexcept { v_ += o.v_; return *this; }
    constexpr Quantity& operator-=(Quantity o) noexcept { v_ -= o.v_; return *this; }
    constexpr Quantity& operator*=(Rep k) noexcept { v_ *= k; return *this; }
    constexpr Quantity& operator/=(Rep k) noexcept { v_ /= k; return *this; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator+(Quantity a, Quantity b) noexcept { return Quantity{a.v_ + b.v_}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator-(Quantity a, Quantity b) noexcept { return Quantity{a.v_ - b.v_}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator-(Quantity a) noexcept { return Quantity{-a.v_}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator*(Quantity a, Rep k) noexcept { return Quantity{a.v_ * k}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator*(Rep k, Quantity a) noexcept { return Quantity{k * a.v_}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr Quantity operator/(Quantity a, Rep k) noexcept { return Quantity{a.v_ / k}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr auto operator<=>(const Quantity&, const Quantity&) noexcept = default;
private:
    Rep v_{};
};
template <int Kg1, int M1, int S1, int K1, int Mol1, int A1, int Cd1,
          int Kg2, int M2, int S2, int K2, int Mol2, int A2, int Cd2, std::floating_point Rep>
[[nodiscard]] constexpr auto operator*(Quantity<Kg1, M1, S1, K1, Mol1, A1, Cd1, Rep> a,
                                       Quantity<Kg2, M2, S2, K2, Mol2, A2, Cd2, Rep> b) noexcept {
    return Quantity<Kg1 + Kg2, M1 + M2, S1 + S2, K1 + K2, Mol1 + Mol2, A1 + A2, Cd1 + Cd2, Rep>{a.value() * b.value()};
}
template <int Kg1, int M1, int S1, int K1, int Mol1, int A1, int Cd1,
          int Kg2, int M2, int S2, int K2, int Mol2, int A2, int Cd2, std::floating_point Rep>
[[nodiscard]] constexpr auto operator/(Quantity<Kg1, M1, S1, K1, Mol1, A1, Cd1, Rep> a,
                                       Quantity<Kg2, M2, S2, K2, Mol2, A2, Cd2, Rep> b) noexcept {
    return Quantity<Kg1 - Kg2, M1 - M2, S1 - S2, K1 - K2, Mol1 - Mol2, A1 - A2, Cd1 - Cd2, Rep>{a.value() / b.value()};
}
/// Square root; only defined when every exponent is even.
template <int Kg, int M, int S, int K, int Mol, int A, int Cd, std::floating_point Rep>
    requires(Kg % 2 == 0 && M % 2 == 0 && S % 2 == 0 && K % 2 == 0 && Mol % 2 == 0 && A % 2 == 0 && Cd % 2 == 0)
[[nodiscard]] inline auto sqrt(Quantity<Kg, M, S, K, Mol, A, Cd, Rep> q) noexcept {
    return Quantity<Kg / 2, M / 2, S / 2, K / 2, Mol / 2, A / 2, Cd / 2, Rep>{std::sqrt(q.value())};
}
//                                   kg  m   s   K  mol A  cd
using Dimensionless       = Quantity<0,  0,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Mass                = Quantity<1,  0,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Length              = Quantity<0,  1,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Time                = Quantity<0,  0,  1,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Temperature         = Quantity<0,  0,  0,  1, 0,  0, 0>;
using Amount              = Quantity<0,  0,  0,  0, 1,  0, 0>;
using Current             = Quantity<0,  0,  0,  0, 0,  1, 0>;
using LuminousIntensity   = Quantity<0,  0,  0,  0, 0,  0, 1>;
using Area                = Quantity<0,  2,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Volume              = Quantity<0,  3,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Velocity            = Quantity<0,  1, -1,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Acceleration        = Quantity<0,  1, -2,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Density             = Quantity<1, -3,  0,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using MassFlow            = Quantity<1,  0, -1,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Pressure            = Quantity<1, -1, -2,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Force               = Quantity<1,  1, -2,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using DynamicViscosity    = Quantity<1, -1, -1,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using KinematicViscosity  = Quantity<0,  2, -1,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Energy              = Quantity<1,  2, -2,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using Power               = Quantity<1,  2, -3,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using HeatFlux            = Quantity<1,  0, -3,  0, 0,  0, 0>;
using SpecificGasConstant = Quantity<0,  2, -2, -1, 0,  0, 0>;
namespace literals {
constexpr Length operator""_m(long double v) noexcept { return Length{static_cast<double>(v)}; }
constexpr Mass operator""_kg(long double v) noexcept { return Mass{static_cast<double>(v)}; }
constexpr Time operator""_s(long double v) noexcept { return Time{static_cast<double>(v)}; }
constexpr Temperature operator""_K(long double v) noexcept { return Temperature{static_cast<double>(v)}; }
constexpr Pressure operator""_Pa(long double v) noexcept { return Pressure{static_cast<double>(v)}; }
}  // namespace literals
// --- compile-time contract ---------------------------------------------------
template <class A, class B> concept Addable = requires(A a, B b) { a + b; };
template <class A, class B> concept Subtractable = requires(A a, B b) { a - b; };
template <class A, class B> concept Comparable = requires(A a, B b) { a < b; };
static_assert(sizeof(Pressure) == sizeof(double), "Quantity must be zero-overhead");
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<Pressure> && std::is_standard_layout_v<Pressure>);
static_assert(Addable<Length, Length> && Addable<Pressure, Pressure>);
static_assert(!Addable<Length, Pressure>, "Length + Pressure must not compile");
static_assert(!Subtractable<Area, Volume> && !Comparable<Velocity, Length>);
static_assert(!Addable<Length, double>, "raw numbers never mix with quantities");
static_assert(!std::convertible_to<double, Length> && !std::convertible_to<Length, double>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(Length{} * Length{}), Area>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(MassFlow{} / (Density{} * Area{})), Velocity>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(Pressure{} * Area{}), Force>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(Density{} * Velocity{} * Length{} / DynamicViscosity{}), Dimensionless>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(Pressure{} / (SpecificGasConstant{} * Temperature{})), Density>);
static_assert(std::same_as<decltype(sqrt(Pressure{} / Density{})), Velocity>);
static_assert((Length{2.0} * Length{3.0}).value() == 6.0);
// --- 7-element SI wire contract ----------------------------------------------
enum class WireKind : std::uint8_t {
    Plain = 0,
    AbsolutePressure = 1,
    GaugePressure = 2,
    ReducedPressurePRgh = 3,
    FirstLayerThickness = 4,
    TargetYPlus = 5,
};
/// 16-byte wire record: SI value, exponents of (kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd), and
/// a semantic kind. Layout-identical to `mphys_core::units::SiWire`.
struct SiWire {
    double value;
    std::array<std::int8_t, 7> dim;
    WireKind kind;
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr bool operator==(const SiWire&, const SiWire&) noexcept = default;
};
static_assert(sizeof(SiWire) == 16 && alignof(SiWire) == 8);
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<SiWire> && std::is_standard_layout_v<SiWire>);
template <int Kg, int M, int S, int K, int Mol, int A, int Cd>
[[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire(Quantity<Kg, M, S, K, Mol, A, Cd> q) noexcept {
    return SiWire{q.value(), Quantity<Kg, M, S, K, Mol, A, Cd>::dim, WireKind::Plain};
}
/// Decodes a plain wire record; empty on any dimension or kind mismatch.
template <class Q>
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::optional<Q> from_wire(const SiWire& w) noexcept {
    if (w.dim != Q::dim || w.kind != WireKind::Plain) return std::nullopt;
    return Q{w.value};
}
// --- pressures that must never be confused -------------------------------------
class GaugePressurePa;
/// Thermodynamic pressure. Invariant: finite and p > 0 Pa. The only pressure
/// accepted by compressible / ideal-gas / Arrhenius laws. Constructing one from
/// a non-positive constant is a compile-time error in constant expressions and
/// throws `std::domain_error` at run time.
class AbsolutePressurePa {
public:
    constexpr explicit AbsolutePressurePa(double pa) : p_(pa) {
        if (!valid(pa)) throw std::domain_error("absolute pressure must be finite and > 0 Pa");
    }
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::optional<AbsolutePressurePa> checked(double pa) noexcept {
        if (!valid(pa)) return std::nullopt;
        return AbsolutePressurePa{pa, Trusted{}};
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Pressure get() const noexcept { return p_; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr GaugePressurePa to_gauge(AbsolutePressurePa reference) const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire() const noexcept { return SiWire{p_.value(), Pressure::dim, WireKind::AbsolutePressure}; }
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::optional<AbsolutePressurePa> from_wire(const SiWire& w) noexcept {
        if (w.dim != Pressure::dim || w.kind != WireKind::AbsolutePressure) return std::nullopt;
        return checked(w.value);
    }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr auto operator<=>(const AbsolutePressurePa&, const AbsolutePressurePa&) noexcept = default;
private:
    struct Trusted {};
    constexpr AbsolutePressurePa(double pa, Trusted) noexcept : p_(pa) {}
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr bool valid(double pa) noexcept {
        return pa > 0.0 && pa <= std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    }
    Pressure p_;
};
/// Pressure relative to an explicit reference; may be negative.
class GaugePressurePa {
public:
    constexpr explicit GaugePressurePa(double pa) noexcept : p_(pa) {}
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Pressure get() const noexcept { return p_; }
    /// The only way to obtain an absolute pressure: name the reference.
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::optional<AbsolutePressurePa> to_absolute(AbsolutePressurePa reference) const noexcept {
        return AbsolutePressurePa::checked((p_ + reference.get()).value());
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire() const noexcept { return SiWire{p_.value(), Pressure::dim, WireKind::GaugePressure}; }
    [[nodiscard]] friend constexpr auto operator<=>(const GaugePressurePa&, const GaugePressurePa&) noexcept = default;
private:
    Pressure p_;
};
constexpr GaugePressurePa AbsolutePressurePa::to_gauge(AbsolutePressurePa reference) const noexcept {
    return GaugePressurePa{(p_ - reference.p_).value()};
}
/// Reduced pressure p_rgh = p - rho g.x used by buoyant solvers.
class ReducedPressurePRgh {
public:
    constexpr explicit ReducedPressurePRgh(double pa) noexcept : p_(pa) {}
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Pressure get() const noexcept { return p_; }
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr ReducedPressurePRgh from_absolute(AbsolutePressurePa p, Density rho,
                                                                     const std::array<Acceleration, 3>& g,
                                                                     const std::array<Length, 3>& x) noexcept {
        return ReducedPressurePRgh{(p.get() - hydrostatic(rho, g, x)).value()};
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::optional<AbsolutePressurePa> to_absolute(Density rho, const std::array<Acceleration, 3>& g,
                                                                          const std::array<Length, 3>& x) const noexcept {
        return AbsolutePressurePa::checked((p_ + hydrostatic(rho, g, x)).value());
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire() const noexcept { return SiWire{p_.value(), Pressure::dim, WireKind::ReducedPressurePRgh}; }
private:
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr Pressure hydrostatic(Density rho, const std::array<Acceleration, 3>& g,
                                                        const std::array<Length, 3>& x) noexcept {
        return rho * (g[0] * x[0] + g[1] * x[1] + g[2] * x[2]);
    }
    Pressure p_;
};
/// Ideal-gas density rho = p / (R T). Only an absolute pressure is accepted.
[[nodiscard]] constexpr Density ideal_gas_density(AbsolutePressurePa p, SpecificGasConstant r, Temperature t) noexcept {
    return p.get() / (r * t);
}
static_assert(std::invocable<decltype(&ideal_gas_density), AbsolutePressurePa, SpecificGasConstant, Temperature>);
static_assert(!std::invocable<decltype(&ideal_gas_density), GaugePressurePa, SpecificGasConstant, Temperature>,
              "a gauge pressure must not reach the ideal-gas law");
static_assert(!std::invocable<decltype(&ideal_gas_density), ReducedPressurePRgh, SpecificGasConstant, Temperature>);
static_assert(!std::invocable<decltype(&ideal_gas_density), Pressure, SpecificGasConstant, Temperature>);
static_assert(!std::invocable<decltype(&ideal_gas_density), double, SpecificGasConstant, Temperature>);
static_assert(!std::convertible_to<GaugePressurePa, AbsolutePressurePa> && !std::convertible_to<AbsolutePressurePa, GaugePressurePa>);
/// True when `AbsolutePressurePa{P}` is a valid constant expression.
template <double P> concept ConstantAbsolutePressure = requires { typename std::bool_constant<(AbsolutePressurePa{P}, true)>; };
static_assert(ConstantAbsolutePressure<101325.0>);
static_assert(!ConstantAbsolutePressure<0.0>, "p = 0 Pa must be rejected at compile time");
static_assert(!ConstantAbsolutePressure<-5.0>);
// --- first-layer thickness vs. y+ target ----------------------------------------
/// Wall-normal height of the first prism layer in metres - what the mesher needs.
class FirstLayerThicknessM {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::optional<FirstLayerThicknessM> checked(double m) noexcept {
        if (!(m > 0.0) || !(m <= std::numeric_limits<double>::max())) return std::nullopt;
        return FirstLayerThicknessM{m};
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr Length get() const noexcept { return h_; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire() const noexcept { return SiWire{h_.value(), Length::dim, WireKind::FirstLayerThickness}; }
private:
    constexpr explicit FirstLayerThicknessM(double m) noexcept : h_(m) {}
    Length h_;
};
/// Reference state needed to turn a y+ target into a length.
struct FlowReference {
    Velocity u_inf;
    Density rho;
    DynamicViscosity mu;
    Length l_ref;
};
struct YPlusEstimate {
    double reynolds;
    double skin_friction;
    Pressure wall_shear;
    Velocity friction_velocity;
    FirstLayerThicknessM first_layer;
};
/// Dimensionless wall-distance target: an estimate request, not a length.
class TargetYPlus {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::optional<TargetYPlus> checked(double y_plus) noexcept {
        if (!(y_plus > 0.0) || !(y_plus <= std::numeric_limits<double>::max())) return std::nullopt;
        return TargetYPlus{y_plus};
    }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr double get() const noexcept { return y_; }
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr SiWire to_wire() const noexcept { return SiWire{y_, Dimensionless::dim, WireKind::TargetYPlus}; }
    /// Flat-plate estimate: Cf = 0.058 Re^-0.2, tau_w = Cf rho U^2 / 2,
    /// u_tau = sqrt(tau_w / rho), dy1 = y+ mu / (rho u_tau).
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<YPlusEstimate> resolve(const FlowReference& r) const noexcept {
        const double in[4] = {r.u_inf.value(), r.rho.value(), r.mu.value(), r.l_ref.value()};
        for (double v : in) {
            if (!(v > 0.0) || !(v <= std::numeric_limits<double>::max())) return std::nullopt;
        }
        const Dimensionless re = r.rho * r.u_inf * r.l_ref / r.mu;
        const double cf = 0.058 * std::pow(re.value(), -0.2);
        const Pressure tau_w = r.rho * r.u_inf * r.u_inf * (0.5 * cf);
        const Velocity u_tau = sqrt(tau_w / r.rho);
        const Length dy1 = r.mu / (r.rho * u_tau) * y_;
        const auto fl = FirstLayerThicknessM::checked(dy1.value());
        if (!fl) return std::nullopt;
        return YPlusEstimate{re.value(), cf, tau_w, u_tau, *fl};
    }
private:
    constexpr explicit TargetYPlus(double y) noexcept : y_(y) {}
    double y_;
};
static_assert(!std::convertible_to<TargetYPlus, FirstLayerThicknessM>, "y+ is not a length");
static_assert(!std::convertible_to<TargetYPlus, Length> && !std::convertible_to<Length, FirstLayerThicknessM>);
static_assert(!std::constructible_from<FirstLayerThicknessM, TargetYPlus>);
}  // namespace mphys

Created core/cpp/include/mphys/mesh_arena.hpp
Created core/cpp/include/mphys/mesh_arena.hpp

// mphys/mesh_arena.hpp - 64-byte aligned, 2 MiB page bump arena (C++20).
//
// Replaces per-cell / per-face std::vector<int> allocations. All connectivity
// of one mesh generation lives in a handful of large pages:
//   * every array is contiguous and starts on a cache-line boundary;
//   * reset() is O(1): it rewinds the cursor and keeps the pages, so repeated
//     AMR cycles never touch the system allocator and cannot fragment the heap;
//   * any array can be handed to C, Rust, RDMA or shared memory as a CAbiSpan.
//
// MeshArena itself is single-threaded by design: use MeshArena::thread_local_arena()
// for scratch data and SharedMeshArena / global_mesh_arena() for data that
// several threads allocate into.
#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <new>
#include <span>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
namespace mphys {
inline constexpr std::size_t kArenaPageBytes = std::size_t{2} << 20;
inline constexpr std::size_t kCacheLine = 64;
inline constexpr std::size_t kArenaPageAlign = 4096;
/// Borrowed, C-ABI compatible view of a contiguous array
/// (layout-identical to `mphys_core::arena::CAbiSpan`).
extern "C" struct CAbiSpan {
    const void* data;
    std::uint64_t len;
    std::uint32_t elem_size;
    std::uint32_t align;
};
static_assert(sizeof(CAbiSpan) == 24 && std::is_standard_layout_v<CAbiSpan> && std::is_trivially_copyable_v<CAbiSpan>);
struct ArenaStats {
    std::size_t pages;
    std::size_t reserved_bytes;
    std::size_t used_bytes;
    std::size_t requested_bytes;
    std::uint64_t allocations;
    std::uint64_t epoch;
};
class MeshArena {
public:
    MeshArena() noexcept = default;
    MeshArena(const MeshArena&) = delete;
    MeshArena& operator=(const MeshArena&) = delete;
    MeshArena(MeshArena&& o) noexcept { swap(o); }
    MeshArena& operator=(MeshArena&& o) noexcept {
        if (this != &o) {
            release();
            swap(o);
        }
        return *this;
    }
    ~MeshArena() { release(); }
    /// Allocates `bytes` aligned to `align` (power of two, at most 4096).
    [[nodiscard]] void* allocate(std::size_t bytes, std::size_t align = kCacheLine) {
        if (align == 0 || (align & (align - 1)) != 0 || align > kArenaPageAlign) throw std::bad_alloc();
        bytes = std::max<std::size_t>(bytes, 1);
        for (;;) {
            if (page_ < pages_.size()) {
                const Page& pg = pages_[page_];
                const std::size_t start = (offset_ + align - 1) & ~(align - 1);
                if (start + bytes <= pg.size) {
                    offset_ = start + bytes;
                    ++allocations_;
                    requested_ += bytes;
                    return pg.ptr + start;
                }
                used_before_ += pg.size;  // skip to the next retained page
                ++page_;
                offset_ = 0;
            } else {
                const std::size_t size = ((bytes + kArenaPageBytes - 1) / kArenaPageBytes) * kArenaPageBytes;
                auto* p = static_cast<std::byte*>(::operator new(size, std::align_val_t{kArenaPageAlign}));
                pages_.push_back(Page{p, size});
                page_ = pages_.size() - 1;
                offset_ = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    /// Allocates a 64-byte aligned array of `n` copies of `fill`.
    template <class T>
        requires std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T> && std::is_trivially_destructible_v<T>
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<T> alloc_span(std::size_t n, const T& fill = T{}) {
        if (n > static_cast<std::size_t>(-1) / sizeof(T)) throw std::bad_alloc();
        T* p = static_cast<T*>(allocate(n * sizeof(T), std::max(alignof(T), kCacheLine)));
        std::uninitialized_fill_n(p, n, fill);
        return {p, n};
    }
    /// O(1): rewinds the cursor and keeps every page. All spans handed out
    /// before the call are invalidated; `epoch()` changes so stale handles can
    /// be detected (see ArenaRef).
    void reset() noexcept {
        page_ = 0;
        offset_ = 0;
        used_before_ = 0;
        allocations_ = 0;
        requested_ = 0;
        ++epoch_;
    }
    /// Returns all pages to the operating system.
    void release() noexcept {
        for (const Page& p : pages_) ::operator delete(p.ptr, std::align_val_t{kArenaPageAlign});
        pages_.clear();
        reset();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t epoch() const noexcept { return epoch_; }
    [[nodiscard]] ArenaStats stats() const noexcept {
        std::size_t reserved = 0;
        for (const Page& p : pages_) reserved += p.size;
        return ArenaStats{pages_.size(), reserved, pages_.empty() ? 0 : used_before_ + offset_, requested_, allocations_, epoch_};
    }
    /// Describes an array for a consumer on the other side of a C ABI.
    template <class T>
    [[nodiscard]] static CAbiSpan export_span(std::span<T> s) noexcept {
        return CAbiSpan{s.data(), s.size(), static_cast<std::uint32_t>(sizeof(T)), static_cast<std::uint32_t>(kCacheLine)};
    }
    /// Per-thread scratch arena.
    [[nodiscard]] static MeshArena& thread_local_arena() {
        thread_local MeshArena arena;
        return arena;
    }
private:
    struct Page {
        std::byte* ptr;
        std::size_t size;
    };
    void swap(MeshArena& o) noexcept {
        std::swap(pages_, o.pages_);
        std::swap(page_, o.page_);
        std::swap(offset_, o.offset_);
        std::swap(used_before_, o.used_before_);
        std::swap(requested_, o.requested_);
        std::swap(allocations_, o.allocations_);
        std::swap(epoch_, o.epoch_);
    }
    std::vector<Page> pages_;
    std::size_t page_ = 0;
    std::size_t offset_ = 0;
    std::size_t used_before_ = 0;
    std::size_t requested_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t allocations_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t epoch_ = 0;
};
/// A span tagged with the arena epoch it was allocated in; `valid()` turns a
/// use-after-reset into a checkable condition.
template <class T>
class ArenaRef {
public:
    ArenaRef() noexcept = default;
    ArenaRef(const MeshArena& arena, std::span<T> s) noexcept : arena_(&arena), span_(s), epoch_(arena.epoch()) {}
    [[nodiscard]] bool valid() const noexcept { return arena_ != nullptr && arena_->epoch() == epoch_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<T> get() const noexcept { return valid() ? span_ : std::span<T>{}; }
private:
    const MeshArena* arena_ = nullptr;
    std::span<T> span_{};
    std::uint64_t epoch_ = 0;
};
/// Mutex-protected arena for allocations coming from several threads.
class SharedMeshArena {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] void* allocate(std::size_t bytes, std::size_t align = kCacheLine) {
        std::scoped_lock lock(m_);
        return arena_.allocate(bytes, align);
    }
    template <class T>
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<T> alloc_span(std::size_t n, const T& fill = T{}) {
        std::scoped_lock lock(m_);
        return arena_.alloc_span<T>(n, fill);
    }
    void reset() noexcept {
        std::scoped_lock lock(m_);
        arena_.reset();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] ArenaStats stats() const noexcept {
        std::scoped_lock lock(m_);
        return arena_.stats();
    }
private:
    mutable std::mutex m_;
    MeshArena arena_;
};
/// Process-wide arena for long-lived mesh data.
[[nodiscard]] inline SharedMeshArena& global_mesh_arena() {
    static SharedMeshArena arena;
    return arena;
}
/// Minimal allocator adaptor so standard containers can live in an arena:
/// `std::vector<int, ArenaAllocator<int>> v{ArenaAllocator<int>{arena}};`
/// Deallocation is a no-op; memory is reclaimed by `MeshArena::reset()`.
template <class T>
class ArenaAllocator {
public:
    using value_type = T;
    explicit ArenaAllocator(MeshArena& arena) noexcept : arena_(&arena) {}
    template <class U>
    ArenaAllocator(const ArenaAllocator<U>& o) noexcept : arena_(o.arena()) {}
    [[nodiscard]] T* allocate(std::size_t n) {
        return static_cast<T*>(arena_->allocate(n * sizeof(T), std::max(alignof(T), kCacheLine)));
    }
    void deallocate(T*, std::size_t) noexcept {}
    [[nodiscard]] MeshArena* arena() const noexcept { return arena_; }
    template <class U>
    [[nodiscard]] bool operator==(const ArenaAllocator<U>& o) const noexcept { return arena_ == o.arena(); }
private:
    MeshArena* arena_;
};
}  // namespace mphys

Created core/cpp/include/mphys/deterministic.hpp
Created core/cpp/include/mphys/deterministic.hpp

// mphys/deterministic.hpp - deterministic parallel building blocks (C++20).
//
// Mesh graphs are assembled as  count -> exclusive scan -> scatter into
// disjoint ranges, and ordered with a *stable* radix sort on 64-bit
// space-filling-curve keys. No unordered atomics, no hash maps: the output is a
// pure function of the input and bit-identical on 1, 8 or 64 threads. Same
// algorithms, same results as the Rust module mphys_core::determinism.
#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <thread>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
#include "mesh_arena.hpp"
namespace mphys {
inline constexpr unsigned kKeyBits = 21;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kKeyMax = (std::uint32_t{1} << kKeyBits) - 1;
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t spread21(std::uint32_t v) noexcept {
    std::uint64_t x = v & 0x1fffffu;
    x = (x | (x << 32)) & 0x001f00000000ffffull;
    x = (x | (x << 16)) & 0x001f0000ff0000ffull;
    x = (x | (x << 8)) & 0x100f00f00f00f00full;
    x = (x | (x << 4)) & 0x10c30c30c30c30c3ull;
    x = (x | (x << 2)) & 0x1249249249249249ull;
    return x;
}
/// 63-bit Morton (Z-order) key of three 21-bit coordinates.
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t morton3(std::uint32_t x, std::uint32_t y, std::uint32_t z) noexcept {
    return spread21(x) | (spread21(y) << 1) | (spread21(z) << 2);
}
/// Hilbert key of three `bits`-bit coordinates (Skilling's transpose algorithm).
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t hilbert3(std::uint32_t x, std::uint32_t y, std::uint32_t z,
                                               unsigned bits = kKeyBits) noexcept {
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 3> c{x, y, z};
    const std::uint32_t m = std::uint32_t{1} << (bits - 1);
    for (std::uint32_t q = m; q > 1; q >>= 1) {
        const std::uint32_t p = q - 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
            if (c[i] & q) {
                c[0] ^= p;
            } else {
                const std::uint32_t t = (c[0] ^ c[i]) & p;
                c[0] ^= t;
                c[i] ^= t;
            }
        }
    }
    for (int i = 1; i < 3; ++i) c[i] ^= c[i - 1];
    std::uint32_t t = 0;
    for (std::uint32_t q = m; q > 1; q >>= 1) {
        if (c[2] & q) t ^= q - 1;
    }
    for (auto& v : c) v ^= t;
    std::uint64_t h = 0;
    for (int b = static_cast<int>(bits) - 1; b >= 0; --b) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) h = (h << 1) | ((c[i] >> b) & 1u);
    }
    return h;
}
static_assert(morton3(1, 0, 0) == 1 && morton3(0, 1, 0) == 2 && morton3(0, 0, 1) == 4);
static_assert(morton3(kKeyMax, kKeyMax, kKeyMax) == (std::uint64_t{1} << 63) - 1);
static_assert(hilbert3(0, 0, 0, 1) == 0 && hilbert3(0, 0, 0) == 0);
/// Quantises a point to the 21-bit lattice spanned by [lo, hi].
[[nodiscard]] inline std::array<std::uint32_t, 3> quantize(const std::array<double, 3>& p, const std::array<double, 3>& lo,
                                                           const std::array<double, 3>& hi) noexcept {
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 3> q{0, 0, 0};
    for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) {
        const double span = hi[a] - lo[a];
        if (span > 0.0) {
            const double t = std::clamp((p[a] - lo[a]) / span * static_cast<double>(kKeyMax), 0.0, static_cast<double>(kKeyMax));
            q[a] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(t);
        }
    }
    return q;
}
namespace detail {
struct Range {
    std::size_t lo, hi;
};
[[nodiscard]] constexpr Range chunk_range(std::size_t n, std::size_t chunks, std::size_t c) noexcept {
    return Range{static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<unsigned __int128>(n) * c / chunks),
                 static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<unsigned __int128>(n) * (c + 1) / chunks)};
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::size_t workers(std::size_t threads, std::size_t n) noexcept {
    return std::min(std::clamp<std::size_t>(threads, 1, 256), std::max<std::size_t>(n, 1));
}
/// Runs f(chunk) for every chunk on its own thread (joined before returning).
template <class F>
void run_chunks(std::size_t n_chunks, F&& f) {
    if (n_chunks <= 1) {
        f(std::size_t{0});
        return;
    }
    std::vector<std::jthread> pool;
    pool.reserve(n_chunks);
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < n_chunks; ++c) pool.emplace_back([&f, c] { f(c); });
}
}  // namespace detail
/// Parallel exclusive prefix sum: out[i] = sum(counts[0..i)), out[n] = total.
/// `out.size()` must be `counts.size() + 1`. Identical for every thread count.
inline void exclusive_scan(std::span<const std::uint32_t> counts, std::span<std::uint32_t> out, std::size_t threads) {
    const std::size_t n = counts.size();
    const std::size_t t = detail::workers(threads, n);
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> sums(t, 0), base(t, 0);
    detail::run_chunks(t, [&](std::size_t c) {
        const auto r = detail::chunk_range(n, t, c);
        std::uint32_t s = 0;
        for (std::size_t i = r.lo; i < r.hi; ++i) s += counts[i];
        sums[c] = s;  // one slot per worker
    });
    std::uint32_t acc = 0;
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < t; ++c) {
        base[c] = acc;
        acc += sums[c];
    }
    out[n] = acc;
    detail::run_chunks(t, [&](std::size_t c) {
        const auto r = detail::chunk_range(n, t, c);
        std::uint32_t a = base[c];
        for (std::size_t i = r.lo; i < r.hi; ++i) {
            out[i] = a;  // disjoint ranges
            a += counts[i];
        }
    });
}
/// Stable parallel LSD radix sort. Fills `perm` with the permutation that
/// sorts `keys`; equal keys keep their input order. Identical for every
/// thread count.
inline void sort_perm_by_key(std::span<const std::uint64_t> keys, std::span<std::uint32_t> perm, std::size_t threads) {
    const std::size_t n = keys.size();
    const std::size_t t = detail::workers(threads, n);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) perm[i] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i);
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> tmp(n), hist(t * 256);
    std::uint64_t all_or = 0, all_and = ~std::uint64_t{0};
    for (auto k : keys) {
        all_or |= k;
        all_and &= k;
    }
    std::uint32_t* src = perm.data();
    std::uint32_t* dst = tmp.data();
    for (unsigned pass = 0; pass < 8; ++pass) {
        const unsigned shift = pass * 8;
        if ((((all_or ^ all_and) >> shift) & 0xff) == 0) continue;  // digit identical for all keys
        std::fill(hist.begin(), hist.end(), 0u);
        detail::run_chunks(t, [&](std::size_t c) {
            const auto r = detail::chunk_range(n, t, c);
            std::uint32_t* h = hist.data() + c * 256;
            for (std::size_t i = r.lo; i < r.hi; ++i) ++h[(keys[src[i]] >> shift) & 0xff];
        });
        std::uint32_t acc = 0;
        for (std::size_t d = 0; d < 256; ++d) {
            for (std::size_t c = 0; c < t; ++c) {
                const std::uint32_t v = hist[c * 256 + d];
                hist[c * 256 + d] = acc;
                acc += v;
            }
        }
        detail::run_chunks(t, [&](std::size_t c) {
            const auto r = detail::chunk_range(n, t, c);
            std::uint32_t* h = hist.data() + c * 256;
            for (std::size_t i = r.lo; i < r.hi; ++i) {
                const std::uint32_t e = src[i];
                dst[h[(keys[e] >> shift) & 0xff]++] = e;  // ranges are disjoint per (digit, chunk)
            }
        });
        std::swap(src, dst);
    }
    if (src != perm.data()) std::copy(src, src + n, perm.data());
}
/// Thread-count independent floating-point sum (fixed 4096-element leaves,
/// fixed pairwise tree).
[[nodiscard]] inline double deterministic_sum(std::span<const double> values, std::size_t threads) {
    constexpr std::size_t leaf = 4096;
    const std::size_t n_leaves = (values.size() + leaf - 1) / leaf;
    if (n_leaves == 0) return 0.0;
    std::vector<double> level(n_leaves, 0.0);
    const std::size_t t = detail::workers(threads, n_leaves);
    detail::run_chunks(t, [&](std::size_t c) {
        const auto r = detail::chunk_range(n_leaves, t, c);
        for (std::size_t l = r.lo; l < r.hi; ++l) {
            const std::size_t lo = l * leaf, hi = std::min(lo + leaf, values.size());
            double s = 0.0;
            for (std::size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) s += values[i];
            level[l] = s;
        }
    });
    while (level.size() > 1) {
        std::vector<double> next;
        next.reserve((level.size() + 1) / 2);
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < level.size(); i += 2) next.push_back(i + 1 < level.size() ? level[i] + level[i + 1] : level[i]);
        level = std::move(next);
    }
    return level[0];
}
/// CSR arrays living in a MeshArena (contiguous, 64-byte aligned, exportable).
struct ArenaCsr {
    std::span<std::uint32_t> offsets;
    std::span<std::uint32_t> cols;
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const std::uint32_t> row(std::size_t r) const noexcept {
        return cols.subspan(offsets[r], offsets[r + 1] - offsets[r]);
    }
};
/// Builds a CSR pattern from unordered (row, col) pairs: stable radix sort on
/// (row << 32) | col, then count -> exclusive scan. Columns end up sorted in
/// every row. Identical for every thread count.
[[nodiscard]] inline ArenaCsr build_csr(MeshArena& arena, std::size_t n_rows,
                                        std::span<const std::pair<std::uint32_t, std::uint32_t>> pairs, std::size_t threads) {
    MeshArena& scratch = MeshArena::thread_local_arena();
    const auto keys = scratch.alloc_span<std::uint64_t>(pairs.size());
    const auto perm = scratch.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(pairs.size());
    const auto counts = scratch.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(n_rows);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pairs.size(); ++i) {
        keys[i] = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(pairs[i].first) << 32) | pairs[i].second;
        ++counts[pairs[i].first];
    }
    sort_perm_by_key(keys, perm, threads);
    ArenaCsr csr{arena.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(n_rows + 1), arena.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(pairs.size())};
    exclusive_scan(counts, csr.offsets, threads);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pairs.size(); ++i) csr.cols[i] = pairs[perm[i]].second;
    scratch.reset();
    return csr;
}
/// Cell -> face CSR from owner/neighbour arrays (the CellFaceCSR layout):
/// one contiguous array instead of one std::vector<int> per cell.
[[nodiscard]] inline ArenaCsr build_cell_face_csr(MeshArena& arena, std::size_t n_cells, std::span<const std::uint32_t> owner,
                                                  std::span<const std::uint32_t> neighbour, std::size_t threads) {
    MeshArena& scratch = MeshArena::thread_local_arena();
    const auto counts = scratch.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(n_cells);
    for (auto o : owner) ++counts[o];
    for (auto n : neighbour) ++counts[n];
    ArenaCsr csr{arena.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(n_cells + 1), arena.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(owner.size() + neighbour.size())};
    exclusive_scan(counts, csr.offsets, threads);
    const auto cursor = scratch.alloc_span<std::uint32_t>(n_cells);
    std::copy_n(csr.offsets.begin(), n_cells, cursor.begin());
    for (std::size_t f = 0; f < owner.size(); ++f) {
        csr.cols[cursor[owner[f]]++] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(f);
        if (f < neighbour.size()) csr.cols[cursor[neighbour[f]]++] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(f);
    }
    scratch.reset();
    return csr;
}
}  // namespace mphys

Created core/cpp/include/mphys/selector.hpp
Created core/cpp/include/mphys/selector.hpp

// mphys/selector.hpp - topology-independent boundary selector DSL (C++20).
//
// Same language and semantics as the Rust module mphys_core::selector:
//
//   normal.dot([-1,0,0]) > 0.995 and abs(x - bbox.x_min) < 1e-4
//   cad_name ~= "flange_inlet_*" or (radius_yz < 0.05 and area > 1e-4)
//   in_cylinder([0,0,0], [0,0,1], 0.05) and not in_sphere([0,0,0.5], 0.1)
//
// compile() type-checks an expression into a fixed-capacity postfix program;
// Program::eval() and bind() run on fixed-size stacks. Neither compilation nor
// evaluation nor binding allocates from the heap.
//
// Ambiguity contract: bind() never guesses. A rule that matches nothing, a
// face group claimed by two rules or (optionally) by none is reported as a
// TopologyRemapConflict carrying the face group id, centroid and bounding box.
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <numbers>
#include <span>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
namespace mphys::selector {
using Vec3 = std::array<double, 3>;
/// Geometric summary of one face group (CAD face / mesh patch).
struct FaceGroup {
    std::uint32_t id = 0;
    std::string_view cad_name{};
    Vec3 centroid{};
    Vec3 normal{};  ///< area-weighted mean unit normal, pointing out of the fluid
    double area = 0.0;
    double curvature = 0.0;  ///< mean curvature magnitude estimate [1/m]
    Vec3 bbox_min{};
    Vec3 bbox_max{};
};
struct ModelContext {
    Vec3 bbox_min{};
    Vec3 bbox_max{};
    [[nodiscard]] static ModelContext from_groups(std::span<const FaceGroup> groups) noexcept {
        ModelContext c;
        if (groups.empty()) return c;
        c.bbox_min.fill(std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());
        c.bbox_max.fill(-std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());
        for (const auto& g : groups) {
            for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) {
                c.bbox_min[a] = std::min(c.bbox_min[a], g.bbox_min[a]);
                c.bbox_max[a] = std::max(c.bbox_max[a], g.bbox_max[a]);
            }
        }
        return c;
    }
};
struct Error {
    std::size_t pos = 0;        ///< byte offset into the selector source
    const char* msg = nullptr;  ///< static message
};
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxOps = 256;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxStack = 32;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxPatterns = 16;
inline constexpr std::size_t kPatternPool = 512;
inline constexpr int kMaxNesting = 64;
[[nodiscard]] constexpr double dot(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) noexcept { return a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2]; }
[[nodiscard]] constexpr Vec3 sub(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b) noexcept { return {a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1], a[2] - b[2]}; }
[[nodiscard]] inline double norm(const Vec3& a) noexcept { return std::sqrt(dot(a, a)); }
/// Signed distance to an axis-aligned box (negative inside).
[[nodiscard]] inline double sdf_box(const Vec3& p, const Vec3& lo, const Vec3& hi) noexcept {
    double outside = 0.0, inside = -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) {
        const double q = std::abs(p[a] - 0.5 * (lo[a] + hi[a])) - 0.5 * (hi[a] - lo[a]);
        outside += std::max(q, 0.0) * std::max(q, 0.0);
        inside = std::max(inside, q);
    }
    return std::sqrt(outside) + std::min(inside, 0.0);
}
[[nodiscard]] inline double sdf_sphere(const Vec3& p, const Vec3& c, double r) noexcept { return norm(sub(p, c)) - r; }
/// Signed distance to a capped cylinder with axis p0 -> p1 (negative inside).
[[nodiscard]] inline double sdf_cylinder(const Vec3& p, const Vec3& p0, const Vec3& p1, double r) noexcept {
    const Vec3 axis = sub(p1, p0);
    const double h = norm(axis);
    if (h == 0.0) return sdf_sphere(p, p0, r);
    const Vec3 d = sub(p, p0);
    const double t = dot(d, axis) / h;
    const double dr = std::sqrt(std::max(dot(d, d) - t * t, 0.0)) - r;
    const double da = std::abs(t - 0.5 * h) - 0.5 * h;
    const double o1 = std::max(dr, 0.0), o2 = std::max(da, 0.0);
    return std::min(std::max(dr, da), 0.0) + std::sqrt(o1 * o1 + o2 * o2);
}
/// Signed distance to the plane through p0 with normal n (positive along n).
[[nodiscard]] inline double sdf_plane(const Vec3& p, const Vec3& p0, const Vec3& n) noexcept {
    const double len = norm(n);
    return len == 0.0 ? std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN() : dot(sub(p, p0), n) / len;
}
/// Allocation-free glob match: `*` matches any run, `?` any single character.
[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool glob_match(std::string_view pat, std::string_view s) noexcept {
    std::size_t p = 0, i = 0, star = std::string_view::npos, mark = 0;
    while (i < s.size()) {
        if (p < pat.size() && pat[p] == '*') {
            star = p++;
            mark = i;
        } else if (p < pat.size() && (pat[p] == '?' || pat[p] == s[i])) {
            ++p;
            ++i;
        } else if (star != std::string_view::npos) {
            p = star + 1;
            i = ++mark;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }
    while (p < pat.size() && pat[p] == '*') ++p;
    return p == pat.size();
}
static_assert(glob_match("flange_inlet_*", "flange_inlet_01") && !glob_match("blade_?", "blade_10"));
namespace detail {
enum class Code : std::uint8_t {
    Num, Field, VecField, MakeVec, Add, Sub, Mul, Div, Neg, VAdd, VSub, VScale, VNeg, Comp, Dot, Norm, AngleDeg,
    Abs, Sqrt, Min, Max, Lt, Le, Gt, Ge, Eq, Ne, And, Or, Not, Glob, StrEq, StrNe, Dist,
    InBox, InSphere, InCyl, SdfBox, SdfSphere, SdfCyl, SdfPlane,
};
class Compiler;
}  // namespace detail
/// A compiled selector: fixed capacity, trivially copyable, no heap storage.
class Program {
public:
    /// Evaluates the predicate for one face group. No heap allocation.
    [[nodiscard]] bool eval(const FaceGroup& g, const ModelContext& ctx) const noexcept {
        using detail::Code;
        std::array<Vec3, kMaxStack> st;
        std::size_t sp = 0;
        const auto t = [](bool b) noexcept { return b ? 1.0 : 0.0; };
        for (std::size_t k = 0; k < n_ops_; ++k) {
            const Op& op = ops_[k];
            switch (op.code) {
                case Code::Num: st[sp++][0] = op.num; break;
                case Code::Field: st[sp++][0] = field(op.arg, g, ctx); break;
                case Code::VecField: st[sp++] = op.arg == 0 ? g.normal : g.centroid; break;
                case Code::MakeVec: { const Vec3 v{st[sp - 3][0], st[sp - 2][0], st[sp - 1][0]}; sp -= 2; st[sp - 1] = v; break; }
                case Code::Add: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] += st[sp][0]; break;
                case Code::Sub: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] -= st[sp][0]; break;
                case Code::Mul: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] *= st[sp][0]; break;
                case Code::Div: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] /= st[sp][0]; break;
                case Code::Min: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = std::fmin(st[sp - 1][0], st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Max: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = std::fmax(st[sp - 1][0], st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Lt: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] < st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Le: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] <= st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Gt: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] > st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Ge: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] >= st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Eq: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] == st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::Ne: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] != st[sp][0]); break;
                case Code::And: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] != 0.0 && st[sp][0] != 0.0); break;
                case Code::Or: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] != 0.0 || st[sp][0] != 0.0); break;
                case Code::Neg: st[sp - 1][0] = -st[sp - 1][0]; break;
                case Code::Abs: st[sp - 1][0] = std::abs(st[sp - 1][0]); break;
                case Code::Sqrt: st[sp - 1][0] = std::sqrt(st[sp - 1][0]); break;
                case Code::Not: st[sp - 1][0] = t(st[sp - 1][0] == 0.0); break;
                case Code::VAdd: --sp; for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) st[sp - 1][a] += st[sp][a]; break;
                case Code::VSub: --sp; for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) st[sp - 1][a] -= st[sp][a]; break;
                case Code::VScale: --sp; for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) st[sp - 1][a] *= st[sp][0]; break;
                case Code::VNeg: for (int a = 0; a < 3; ++a) st[sp - 1][a] = -st[sp - 1][a]; break;
                case Code::Comp: st[sp - 1][0] = st[sp - 1][op.arg]; break;
                case Code::Dot: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = dot(st[sp - 1], st[sp]); break;
                case Code::Norm: st[sp - 1][0] = norm(st[sp - 1]); break;
                case Code::AngleDeg: {
                    --sp;
                    const double c = dot(st[sp - 1], st[sp]) / (norm(st[sp - 1]) * norm(st[sp]));
                    st[sp - 1][0] = std::acos(std::clamp(c, -1.0, 1.0)) * (180.0 / std::numbers::pi);
                    break;
                }
                case Code::Glob: st[sp++][0] = t(glob_match(pattern(op.arg), g.cad_name)); break;
                case Code::StrEq: st[sp++][0] = t(pattern(op.arg) == g.cad_name); break;
                case Code::StrNe: st[sp++][0] = t(pattern(op.arg) != g.cad_name); break;
                case Code::Dist: st[sp - 1][0] = norm(sub(g.centroid, st[sp - 1])); break;
                case Code::InBox:
                case Code::SdfBox: {
                    --sp;
                    const double d = sdf_box(g.centroid, st[sp - 1], st[sp]);
                    st[sp - 1][0] = op.code == Code::InBox ? t(d <= 0.0) : d;
                    break;
                }
                case Code::InSphere:
                case Code::SdfSphere: {
                    --sp;
                    const double d = sdf_sphere(g.centroid, st[sp - 1], st[sp][0]);
                    st[sp - 1][0] = op.code == Code::InSphere ? t(d <= 0.0) : d;
                    break;
                }
                case Code::InCyl:
                case Code::SdfCyl: {
                    sp -= 2;
                    const double d = sdf_cylinder(g.centroid, st[sp - 1], st[sp], st[sp + 1][0]);
                    st[sp - 1][0] = op.code == Code::InCyl ? t(d <= 0.0) : d;
                    break;
                }
                case Code::SdfPlane: --sp; st[sp - 1][0] = sdf_plane(g.centroid, st[sp - 1], st[sp]); break;
            }
        }
        return sp == 1 && st[0][0] != 0.0;
    }
    /// Evaluates the predicate at a point (refinement zones).
    [[nodiscard]] bool eval_point(const Vec3& p, const ModelContext& ctx) const noexcept {
        FaceGroup g;
        g.centroid = g.bbox_min = g.bbox_max = p;
        return eval(g, ctx);
    }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t instruction_count() const noexcept { return n_ops_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t max_stack() const noexcept { return max_stack_; }
private:
    friend class detail::Compiler;
    struct Op {
        detail::Code code;
        std::uint8_t arg;
        double num;
    };
    [[nodiscard]] static double field(std::uint8_t f, const FaceGroup& g, const ModelContext& ctx) noexcept {
        const Vec3& c = g.centroid;
        if (f <= 2) return c[f];
        if (f <= 5) return g.normal[f - 3];
        switch (f) {
            case 6: return g.area;
            case 7: return g.curvature;
            case 8: return std::sqrt(c[0] * c[0] + c[1] * c[1]);
            case 9: return std::sqrt(c[1] * c[1] + c[2] * c[2]);
            case 10: return std::sqrt(c[0] * c[0] + c[2] * c[2]);
            default: break;
        }
        if (f <= 13) return ctx.bbox_min[f - 11];
        if (f <= 16) return ctx.bbox_max[f - 14];
        if (f <= 19) return g.bbox_min[f - 17];
        return g.bbox_max[std::min<std::size_t>(f - 20, 2)];
    }
    [[nodiscard]] std::string_view pattern(std::size_t i) const noexcept {
        return {pool_.data() + patterns_[i].first, patterns_[i].second};
    }
    std::array<Op, kMaxOps> ops_{};
    std::size_t n_ops_ = 0;
    std::size_t max_stack_ = 0;
    std::array<char, kPatternPool> pool_{};
    std::size_t pool_used_ = 0;
    std::array<std::pair<std::uint16_t, std::uint16_t>, kMaxPatterns> patterns_{};
    std::size_t n_patterns_ = 0;
};
struct CompileResult {
    bool ok = false;
    Program program{};
    Error error{};
};
namespace detail {
enum class Ty : std::uint8_t { Num, Vec, Bool, StrField, StrLit, Err };
enum class T : std::uint8_t {
    Num, Str, Ident, LParen, RParen, LBrack, RBrack, Comma, Dot, Plus, Minus, Star, Slash,
    Lt, Le, Gt, Ge, EqEq, Ne, Match, And, Or, Not, Eof, Bad,
};
struct Func {
    std::string_view name;
    std::array<Ty, 3> params;
    int n;
    Ty ret;
    Code op;
};
inline constexpr std::array<Func, 15> kFuncs{{
    {"abs", {Ty::Num, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 1, Ty::Num, Code::Abs},
    {"sqrt", {Ty::Num, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 1, Ty::Num, Code::Sqrt},
    {"min", {Ty::Num, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::Min},
    {"max", {Ty::Num, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::Max},
    {"dot", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::Dot},
    {"norm", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 1, Ty::Num, Code::Norm},
    {"angle_deg", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::AngleDeg},
    {"dist", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 1, Ty::Num, Code::Dist},
    {"in_box", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Bool, Code::InBox},
    {"in_sphere", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Bool, Code::InSphere},
    {"in_cylinder", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 3, Ty::Bool, Code::InCyl},
    {"sdf_box", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::SdfBox},
    {"sdf_sphere", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Num, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::SdfSphere},
    {"sdf_cylinder", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 3, Ty::Num, Code::SdfCyl},
    {"sdf_plane", {Ty::Vec, Ty::Vec, Ty::Num}, 2, Ty::Num, Code::SdfPlane},
}};
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 11> kNumFields{"x", "y", "z", "nx", "ny", "nz", "area", "curvature",
                                                             "radius_xy", "radius_yz", "radius_xz"};
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 6> kBoxMembers{"x_min", "y_min", "z_min", "x_max", "y_max", "z_max"};
/// Single-pass, allocation-free, type-checking recursive-descent compiler.
class Compiler {
public:
    Compiler(std::string_view src, Program& p) noexcept : s_(src), p_(p) { next(); }
    [[nodiscard]] bool run() noexcept {
        const Ty t = or_expr();
        if (failed_) return false;
        if (tok_ != T::Eof) return fail(pos_, "unexpected trailing input"), false;
        if (t != Ty::Bool) return fail(0, "a selector must be a boolean predicate"), false;
        p_.max_stack_ = static_cast<std::size_t>(max_depth_);
        return true;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] Error error() const noexcept { return err_; }
private:
    static constexpr bool digit(char c) noexcept { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; }
    static constexpr bool alpha(char c) noexcept { return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || c == '_'; }
    [[nodiscard]] char at(std::size_t k) const noexcept { return k < s_.size() ? s_[k] : '\0'; }
    Ty fail(std::size_t pos, const char* msg) noexcept {
        if (!failed_) {
            failed_ = true;
            err_ = Error{pos, msg};
        }
        return Ty::Err;
    }
    void next() noexcept {
        while (i_ < s_.size() && (s_[i_] == ' ' || s_[i_] == '\t' || s_[i_] == '\n' || s_[i_] == '\r')) ++i_;
        pos_ = i_;
        if (i_ >= s_.size()) {
            tok_ = T::Eof;
            return;
        }
        const char c = s_[i_];
        const auto one = [&](T t) { tok_ = t; ++i_; };
        const auto two = [&](T t) { tok_ = t; i_ += 2; };
        const auto bad = [&](const char* m) { tok_ = T::Bad; fail(pos_, m); };
        switch (c) {
            case '(': return one(T::LParen);
            case ')': return one(T::RParen);
            case '[': return one(T::LBrack);
            case ']': return one(T::RBrack);
            case ',': return one(T::Comma);
            case '+': return one(T::Plus);
            case '-': return one(T::Minus);
            case '*': return one(T::Star);
            case '/': return one(T::Slash);
            case '<': return at(i_ + 1) == '=' ? two(T::Le) : one(T::Lt);
            case '>': return at(i_ + 1) == '=' ? two(T::Ge) : one(T::Gt);
            case '=': return at(i_ + 1) == '=' ? two(T::EqEq) : bad("single `=` is not an operator; use `==`");
            case '!': return at(i_ + 1) == '=' ? two(T::Ne) : one(T::Not);
            case '~': return at(i_ + 1) == '=' ? two(T::Match) : bad("expected `~=`");
            case '&': return at(i_ + 1) == '&' ? two(T::And) : bad("expected `&&` or `||`");
            case '|': return at(i_ + 1) == '|' ? two(T::Or) : bad("expected `&&` or `||`");
            case '"':
            case '\'': {
                const std::size_t st = ++i_;
                while (i_ < s_.size() && s_[i_] != c) ++i_;
                if (i_ >= s_.size()) return bad("unterminated string literal");
                text_ = s_.substr(st, i_ - st);
                ++i_;
                tok_ = T::Str;
                return;
            }
            default: break;
        }
        if (digit(c) || (c == '.' && digit(at(i_ + 1)))) {
            const std::size_t st = i_;
            while (digit(at(i_))) ++i_;
            if (at(i_) == '.' && !alpha(at(i_ + 1))) {
                ++i_;
                while (digit(at(i_))) ++i_;
            }
            if (at(i_) == 'e' || at(i_) == 'E') {
                std::size_t j = i_ + 1;
                if (at(j) == '+' || at(j) == '-') ++j;
                if (digit(at(j))) {
                    i_ = j;
                    while (digit(at(i_))) ++i_;
                }
            }
            const auto r = std::from_chars(s_.data() + st, s_.data() + i_, num_);
            if (r.ec != std::errc{} || r.ptr != s_.data() + i_) return bad("invalid number");
            tok_ = T::Num;
            return;
        }
        if (c == '.') return one(T::Dot);
        if (alpha(c)) {
            const std::size_t st = i_;
            while (alpha(at(i_)) || digit(at(i_))) ++i_;
            text_ = s_.substr(st, i_ - st);
            tok_ = text_ == "and" ? T::And : text_ == "or" ? T::Or : text_ == "not" ? T::Not : T::Ident;
            return;
        }
        bad("unexpected character");
    }
    bool emit(Code code, int pops, int pushes, std::uint8_t arg = 0, double num = 0.0) noexcept {
        depth_ += pushes - pops;
        max_depth_ = std::max(max_depth_, depth_);
        if (max_depth_ > static_cast<int>(kMaxStack)) return fail(pos_, "expression needs too many stack slots"), false;
        if (p_.n_ops_ >= kMaxOps) return fail(pos_, "expression has too many instructions"), false;
        p_.ops_[p_.n_ops_++] = Program::Op{code, arg, num};
        return true;
    }
    bool expect(T t, const char* msg) noexcept {
        if (tok_ != t) return fail(pos_, msg), false;
        next();
        return !failed_;
    }
    Ty or_expr() noexcept {
        Ty l = and_expr();
        while (!failed_ && tok_ == T::Or) {
            if (l != Ty::Bool) return fail(pos_, "left operand of `or` must be boolean");
            next();
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            if (and_expr() != Ty::Bool) return fail(p, "right operand of `or` must be boolean");
            if (!emit(Code::Or, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
        }
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : l;
    }
    Ty and_expr() noexcept {
        Ty l = not_expr();
        while (!failed_ && tok_ == T::And) {
            if (l != Ty::Bool) return fail(pos_, "left operand of `and` must be boolean");
            next();
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            if (not_expr() != Ty::Bool) return fail(p, "right operand of `and` must be boolean");
            if (!emit(Code::And, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
        }
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : l;
    }
    Ty not_expr() noexcept {
        if (++nest_ > kMaxNesting) return fail(pos_, "expression is nested too deeply");
        Ty r;
        if (tok_ == T::Not) {
            next();
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            r = not_expr() == Ty::Bool ? Ty::Bool : fail(p, "operand of `not` must be boolean");
            if (r == Ty::Bool && !emit(Code::Not, 1, 1)) r = Ty::Err;
        } else {
            r = cmp_expr();
        }
        --nest_;
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : r;
    }
    Ty cmp_expr() noexcept {
        const Ty l = sum();
        if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
        const std::size_t p = pos_;
        Code rel;
        bool is_match = false;
        switch (tok_) {
            case T::Lt: rel = Code::Lt; break;
            case T::Le: rel = Code::Le; break;
            case T::Gt: rel = Code::Gt; break;
            case T::Ge: rel = Code::Ge; break;
            case T::EqEq: rel = Code::Eq; break;
            case T::Ne: rel = Code::Ne; break;
            case T::Match: rel = Code::Glob; is_match = true; break;
            default: return l;
        }
        next();
        const Ty r = sum();
        if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
        if (l == Ty::Num && r == Ty::Num && !is_match) return emit(rel, 2, 1) ? Ty::Bool : Ty::Err;
        if (l == Ty::StrField && r == Ty::StrLit) {
            const Code c = is_match ? Code::Glob : rel == Code::Eq ? Code::StrEq : rel == Code::Ne ? Code::StrNe : Code::Num;
            if (c == Code::Num) return fail(p, "strings support only `==`, `!=` and `~=`");
            return emit(c, 0, 1, last_lit_) ? Ty::Bool : Ty::Err;
        }
        if (is_match) return fail(p, "`~=` needs `cad_name` on the left and a string pattern on the right");
        return fail(p, "operands cannot be compared");
    }
    Ty sum() noexcept {
        Ty l = term();
        while (!failed_ && (tok_ == T::Plus || tok_ == T::Minus)) {
            const bool plus = tok_ == T::Plus;
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            next();
            const Ty r = term();
            if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
            if (l == Ty::Num && r == Ty::Num) {
                if (!emit(plus ? Code::Add : Code::Sub, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else if (l == Ty::Vec && r == Ty::Vec) {
                if (!emit(plus ? Code::VAdd : Code::VSub, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else {
                return fail(p, "operands cannot be added or subtracted");
            }
        }
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : l;
    }
    Ty term() noexcept {
        Ty l = unary();
        while (!failed_ && (tok_ == T::Star || tok_ == T::Slash)) {
            const bool mul = tok_ == T::Star;
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            next();
            const Ty r = unary();
            if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
            if (l == Ty::Num && r == Ty::Num) {
                if (!emit(mul ? Code::Mul : Code::Div, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else if (l == Ty::Vec && r == Ty::Num && mul) {
                if (!emit(Code::VScale, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else {
                return fail(p, "operands cannot be multiplied or divided (write `vector * scalar`)");
            }
        }
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : l;
    }
    Ty unary() noexcept {
        if (tok_ != T::Minus) return postfix();
        if (++nest_ > kMaxNesting) return fail(pos_, "expression is nested too deeply");
        const std::size_t p = pos_;
        next();
        Ty t = unary();
        --nest_;
        if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
        if (t == Ty::Num) return emit(Code::Neg, 1, 1) ? t : Ty::Err;
        if (t == Ty::Vec) return emit(Code::VNeg, 1, 1) ? t : Ty::Err;
        return fail(p, "operand cannot be negated");
    }
    Ty postfix() noexcept {
        Ty t = primary();
        while (!failed_ && tok_ == T::Dot) {
            const std::size_t p = pos_;
            next();
            if (tok_ != T::Ident) return fail(p, "expected a member name after `.`");
            const std::string_view name = text_;
            if (t != Ty::Vec) return fail(p, "members are only defined on vectors");
            next();
            if (name == "x" || name == "y" || name == "z") {
                if (!emit(Code::Comp, 1, 1, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(name[0] - 'x'))) return Ty::Err;
            } else if (name == "dot") {
                if (!expect(T::LParen, "expected `(`")) return Ty::Err;
                const std::size_t pa = pos_;
                if (or_expr() != Ty::Vec) return fail(pa, "argument of `.dot()` must be a vector");
                if (!expect(T::RParen, "expected `)`") || !emit(Code::Dot, 2, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else if (name == "norm") {
                if (!expect(T::LParen, "expected `(`") || !expect(T::RParen, "expected `)`") || !emit(Code::Norm, 1, 1)) return Ty::Err;
            } else {
                return fail(p, "unknown vector member (use .x .y .z .dot(v) .norm())");
            }
            t = Ty::Num;
        }
        return failed_ ? Ty::Err : t;
    }
    Ty primary() noexcept {
        const std::size_t p = pos_;
        switch (tok_) {
            case T::Num: {
                const double v = num_;
                next();
                return emit(Code::Num, 0, 1, 0, v) ? Ty::Num : Ty::Err;
            }
            case T::Str: {
                if (p_.n_patterns_ >= kMaxPatterns || p_.pool_used_ + text_.size() > kPatternPool) return fail(p, "too many or too long string literals");
                std::copy(text_.begin(), text_.end(), p_.pool_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(p_.pool_used_));
                p_.patterns_[p_.n_patterns_] = {static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p_.pool_used_), static_cast<std::uint16_t>(text_.size())};
                p_.pool_used_ += text_.size();
                last_lit_ = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(p_.n_patterns_++);
                next();
                return Ty::StrLit;
            }
            case T::LParen: {
                next();
                const Ty t = or_expr();
                return expect(T::RParen, "expected `)`") ? t : Ty::Err;
            }
            case T::LBrack: {
                next();
                for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
                    const std::size_t pc = pos_;
                    const Ty t = or_expr();
                    if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
                    if (t != Ty::Num) return fail(pc, "vector components must be numbers");
                    if (k < 2 && !expect(T::Comma, "expected `,` (vectors have exactly 3 components)")) return Ty::Err;
                }
                if (!expect(T::RBrack, "expected `]` (vectors have exactly 3 components)")) return Ty::Err;
                return emit(Code::MakeVec, 3, 1) ? Ty::Vec : Ty::Err;
            }
            case T::Ident: {
                const std::string_view name = text_;
                next();
                return ident(name, p);
            }
            case T::Eof: return fail(p, "unexpected end of selector");
            default: return fail(p, "unexpected token");
        }
    }
    Ty ident(std::string_view name, std::size_t p) noexcept {
        if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
        if (tok_ == T::LParen) {
            const Func* fn = nullptr;
            for (const auto& f : kFuncs) {
                if (f.name == name) fn = &f;
            }
            if (fn == nullptr) return fail(p, "unknown function");
            next();
            for (int k = 0; k < fn->n; ++k) {
                if (k > 0 && !expect(T::Comma, "expected `,` (wrong number of arguments)")) return Ty::Err;
                const std::size_t pa = pos_;
                const Ty t = or_expr();
                if (failed_) return Ty::Err;
                if (t != fn->params[static_cast<std::size_t>(k)]) return fail(pa, "argument has the wrong type");
            }
            if (!expect(T::RParen, "expected `)` (wrong number of arguments)")) return Ty::Err;
            return emit(fn->op, fn->n, 1) ? fn->ret : Ty::Err;
        }
        if (name == "normal") return emit(Code::VecField, 0, 1, 0) ? Ty::Vec : Ty::Err;
        if (name == "centroid") return emit(Code::VecField, 0, 1, 1) ? Ty::Vec : Ty::Err;
        if (name == "cad_name") return Ty::StrField;
        if (name == "true" || name == "false") return emit(Code::Num, 0, 1, 0, name == "true" ? 1.0 : 0.0) ? Ty::Bool : Ty::Err;
        if (name == "bbox" || name == "face") {
            if (!expect(T::Dot, "expected `.` after `bbox` / `face`")) return Ty::Err;
            if (tok_ != T::Ident) return fail(pos_, "expected x_min, y_min, z_min, x_max, y_max or z_max");
            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < kBoxMembers.size(); ++k) {
                if (kBoxMembers[k] == text_) {
                    next();
                    return emit(Code::Field, 0, 1, static_cast<std::uint8_t>((name == "bbox" ? 11 : 17) + k)) ? Ty::Num : Ty::Err;
                }
            }
            return fail(pos_, "unknown bounding-box member");
        }
        for (std::size_t k = 0; k < kNumFields.size(); ++k) {
            if (kNumFields[k] == name) return emit(Code::Field, 0, 1, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(k)) ? Ty::Num : Ty::Err;
        }
        return fail(p, "unknown identifier");
    }
    std::string_view s_;
    Program& p_;
    std::size_t i_ = 0, pos_ = 0;
    T tok_ = T::Eof;
    double num_ = 0.0;
    std::string_view text_{};
    Error err_{};
    bool failed_ = false;
    int depth_ = 0, max_depth_ = 0, nest_ = 0;
    std::uint8_t last_lit_ = 0;
};
}  // namespace detail
/// Compiles and type-checks a selector. Allocation-free; string patterns are
/// copied into the program, so `src` need not outlive the result.
[[nodiscard]] inline CompileResult compile(std::string_view src) noexcept {
    CompileResult r;
    detail::Compiler c(src, r.program);
    r.ok = c.run();
    if (!r.ok) r.error = c.error();
    return r;
}
// --- binding with the strict ambiguity contract --------------------------------
enum class ConflictKind : std::uint8_t { ZeroMatch, Overlap, Unbound };
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kNone = 0xffffffffu;
/// One offending (or candidate) face group of a binding conflict.
struct TopologyRemapConflict {
    ConflictKind kind;
    std::uint32_t rule;           ///< rule index, kNone for Unbound
    std::uint32_t other_rule;     ///< second rule for Overlap, else kNone
    std::uint32_t face_group_id;  ///< offending group; for ZeroMatch a currently unbound candidate (kNone if none)
    Vec3 centroid;
    Vec3 bbox_min;
    Vec3 bbox_max;
};
struct Rule {
    std::string_view name;
    const Program* program;
};
struct BindResult {
    std::size_t conflicts = 0;  ///< total number of conflict records produced
    bool truncated = false;     ///< `out` was too small to hold all of them
    [[nodiscard]] bool ok() const noexcept { return conflicts == 0; }
};
/// Binds rules to face groups. `rule_of_group[i]` receives the rule index of
/// group i (kNone if unbound). Conflicts are written to `out`; nothing is ever
/// resolved by guessing. Performs no heap allocation.
[[nodiscard]] inline BindResult bind(std::span<const Rule> rules, std::span<const FaceGroup> groups, const ModelContext& ctx,
                                     std::span<std::uint32_t> rule_of_group, std::span<TopologyRemapConflict> out,
                                     bool require_full_coverage = true) noexcept {
    BindResult res;
    const auto push = [&](ConflictKind k, std::uint32_t a, std::uint32_t b, const FaceGroup* g) noexcept {
        if (res.conflicts < out.size()) {
            out[res.conflicts] = g ? TopologyRemapConflict{k, a, b, g->id, g->centroid, g->bbox_min, g->bbox_max}
                                   : TopologyRemapConflict{k, a, b, kNone, {}, {}, {}};
        } else {
            res.truncated = true;
        }
        ++res.conflicts;
    };
    for (std::size_t gi = 0; gi < groups.size(); ++gi) {
        rule_of_group[gi] = kNone;
        for (std::size_t a = 0; a < rules.size(); ++a) {
            if (!rules[a].program->eval(groups[gi], ctx)) continue;
            if (rule_of_group[gi] == kNone) rule_of_group[gi] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a);
            for (std::size_t b = a + 1; b < rules.size(); ++b) {
                if (rules[b].program->eval(groups[gi], ctx)) {
                    push(ConflictKind::Overlap, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a), static_cast<std::uint32_t>(b), &groups[gi]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for (std::size_t r = 0; r < rules.size(); ++r) {
        bool any = false;
        for (std::size_t gi = 0; gi < groups.size() && !any; ++gi) any = rules[r].program->eval(groups[gi], ctx);
        if (any) continue;
        bool candidate = false;
        for (std::size_t gi = 0; gi < groups.size(); ++gi) {
            if (rule_of_group[gi] == kNone) {
                push(ConflictKind::ZeroMatch, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(r), kNone, &groups[gi]);
                candidate = true;
            }
        }
        if (!candidate) push(ConflictKind::ZeroMatch, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(r), kNone, nullptr);
    }
    if (require_full_coverage) {
        for (std::size_t gi = 0; gi < groups.size(); ++gi) {
            if (rule_of_group[gi] == kNone) push(ConflictKind::Unbound, kNone, kNone, &groups[gi]);
        }
    }
    return res;
}
}  // namespace mphys::selector

Created core/cpp/include/mphys/flight_recorder.hpp
Created core/cpp/include/mphys/flight_recorder.hpp

// mphys/flight_recorder.hpp - deterministic crash/replay flight recorder (C++20).
//
// When a geometric or numerical invariant fails (FaceClass::INVALID,
// AMIPS > 1e4, n_nan_cells > 0) the recorder writes a compact crash_replay.toml:
// the manifest (verbatim) and its SHA-256, the deterministic seed, the thread
// count, the pipeline stages reached, the offending cell/face coordinates and a
// stack trace. The document uses the schema "mphys.crash_replay/1" and is read
// back by the Rust core (mphys_core::flight::ReplayCase::parse).
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <ctime>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <limits>
#include <span>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
#if __has_include(<execinfo.h>)
#include <execinfo.h>
#define MPHYS_HAVE_EXECINFO 1
#endif
namespace mphys {
/// Classification of a face during mesh graph construction.
enum class FaceClass : std::uint8_t { Interior = 0, Boundary = 1, Interface = 2, INVALID = 255 };
inline constexpr double kAmipsLimit = 1.0e4;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxListedViolations = 32;
enum class ViolationKind : std::uint8_t { InvalidFaceClass, AmipsExceeded, NanCell };
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::string_view kind_name(ViolationKind k) noexcept {
    switch (k) {
        case ViolationKind::InvalidFaceClass: return "invalid_face_class";
        case ViolationKind::AmipsExceeded: return "amips_exceeded";
        case ViolationKind::NanCell: return "nan_cell";
    }
    return "?";
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::string_view invariant_text(ViolationKind k) noexcept {
    switch (k) {
        case ViolationKind::InvalidFaceClass: return "FaceClass != INVALID";
        case ViolationKind::AmipsExceeded: return "AMIPS <= 1e4";
        case ViolationKind::NanCell: return "n_nan_cells == 0";
    }
    return "?";
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::string_view entity_name(ViolationKind k) noexcept {
    return k == ViolationKind::InvalidFaceClass ? "face" : "cell";
}
/// One failed invariant with the location needed for highlighting.
struct Violation {
    ViolationKind kind;
    std::uint32_t index;  ///< cell or face index
    double value;         ///< AMIPS energy, field value or face class code
    std::array<double, 3> coordinates;
};
/// AMIPS energy of a tetrahedron relative to the regular tetrahedron:
/// tr(J^T J) / det(J)^(2/3); 3 for a regular element, +inf if inverted.
[[nodiscard]] inline double amips_tet(const std::array<std::array<double, 3>, 4>& p) noexcept {
    constexpr double A = 0.5773502691896258;  // 1/sqrt(3)
    constexpr double B = 0.408248290463863;   // 1/sqrt(6)
    constexpr double C = 1.224744871391589;   // sqrt(3/2)
    double j[3][3];
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
        const double e0 = p[1][k] - p[0][k], e1 = p[2][k] - p[0][k], e2 = p[3][k] - p[0][k];
        j[0][k] = e0;
        j[1][k] = (2.0 * e1 - e0) * A;
        j[2][k] = e2 * C - (e0 + e1) * B;
    }
    const double det = j[0][0] * (j[1][1] * j[2][2] - j[1][2] * j[2][1]) - j[0][1] * (j[1][0] * j[2][2] - j[1][2] * j[2][0]) +
                       j[0][2] * (j[1][0] * j[2][1] - j[1][1] * j[2][0]);
    if (!(det > 0.0) || !(det <= std::numeric_limits<double>::max())) return std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    double frob = 0.0;
    for (auto& row : j) {
        for (double x : row) frob += x * x;
    }
    return frob / std::pow(det, 2.0 / 3.0);
}
namespace detail {
/// FIPS 180-4 SHA-256, lower-case hex.
[[nodiscard]] inline std::string sha256_hex(std::string_view data) {
    static constexpr std::uint32_t K[64] = {
        0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5, 0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be,
        0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174, 0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa,
        0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da, 0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967, 0x27b70a85,
        0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85, 0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3,
        0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070, 0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f,
        0x682e6ff3, 0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2};
    std::uint32_t h[8] = {0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19};
    std::string msg(data);
    const std::uint64_t bits = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(data.size()) * 8;
    msg.push_back(static_cast<char>(0x80));
    while (msg.size() % 64 != 56) msg.push_back('\0');
    for (int i = 7; i >= 0; --i) msg.push_back(static_cast<char>((bits >> (8 * i)) & 0xff));
    const auto rotr = [](std::uint32_t x, int n) { return (x >> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
    for (std::size_t off = 0; off < msg.size(); off += 64) {
        std::uint32_t w[64];
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
            const auto* b = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(msg.data() + off + 4 * i);
            w[i] = (std::uint32_t{b[0]} << 24) | (std::uint32_t{b[1]} << 16) | (std::uint32_t{b[2]} << 8) | b[3];
        }
        for (int i = 16; i < 64; ++i) {
            const std::uint32_t s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3);
            const std::uint32_t s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10);
            w[i] = w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1;
        }
        std::uint32_t v[8];
        std::copy(h, h + 8, v);
        for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
            const std::uint32_t s1 = rotr(v[4], 6) ^ rotr(v[4], 11) ^ rotr(v[4], 25);
            const std::uint32_t ch = (v[4] & v[5]) ^ (~v[4] & v[6]);
            const std::uint32_t t1 = v[7] + s1 + ch + K[i] + w[i];
            const std::uint32_t s0 = rotr(v[0], 2) ^ rotr(v[0], 13) ^ rotr(v[0], 22);
            const std::uint32_t maj = (v[0] & v[1]) ^ (v[0] & v[2]) ^ (v[1] & v[2]);
            const std::uint32_t t2 = s0 + maj;
            v[7] = v[6]; v[6] = v[5]; v[5] = v[4]; v[4] = v[3] + t1;
            v[3] = v[2]; v[2] = v[1]; v[1] = v[0]; v[0] = t1 + t2;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h[i] += v[i];
    }
    std::string out;
    char buf[9];
    for (std::uint32_t x : h) {
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "%08x", x);
        out += buf;
    }
    return out;
}
[[nodiscard]] inline bool is_control(char c) noexcept {
    const auto u = static_cast<unsigned char>(c);
    return u < 0x20 || u == 0x7f;
}
inline void escape_into(std::string& out, char c, bool keep_newlines) {
    switch (c) {
        case '"': out += "\\\""; return;
        case '\\': out += "\\\\"; return;
        case '\n': out += keep_newlines ? "\n" : "\\n"; return;
        case '\t': out += keep_newlines ? "\t" : "\\t"; return;
        case '\r': out += "\\r"; return;
        default: break;
    }
    if (is_control(c)) {
        char buf[8];
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "\\u%04x", static_cast<unsigned>(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
        out += buf;
    } else {
        out.push_back(c);
    }
}
/// Single-line TOML basic string.
[[nodiscard]] inline std::string toml_quote(std::string_view s) {
    std::string out = "\"";
    for (char c : s) escape_into(out, c, false);
    out.push_back('"');
    return out;
}
/// Multi-line TOML string: verbatim ''' literal when possible, escaped """ otherwise.
[[nodiscard]] inline std::string toml_multiline(std::string_view s) {
    bool verbatim = s.find("'''") == std::string_view::npos && (s.empty() || s.back() != '\'');
    for (char c : s) verbatim = verbatim && (c == '\n' || c == '\t' || !is_control(c));
    if (verbatim) return "'''\n" + std::string(s) + "'''";
    std::string out = "\"\"\"\n";
    for (char c : s) escape_into(out, c, true);
    out += "\"\"\"";
    return out;
}
/// Shortest round-trip TOML float literal.
[[nodiscard]] inline std::string toml_float(double v) {
    if (std::isnan(v)) return "nan";
    if (std::isinf(v)) return v > 0 ? "inf" : "-inf";
    char buf[40];
    const auto r = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof buf, v);
    std::string s(buf, r.ptr);
    if (s.find_first_of(".e") == std::string::npos) s += ".0";
    return s;
}
}  // namespace detail
/// Thrown by FlightRecorder::guard after the replay file has been written.
class InvariantError : public std::runtime_error {
public:
    InvariantError(const std::string& what, std::vector<Violation> v, std::string replay, std::filesystem::path path)
        : std::runtime_error(what), violations(std::move(v)), replay_toml(std::move(replay)), replay_path(std::move(path)) {}
    std::vector<Violation> violations;
    std::string replay_toml;
    std::filesystem::path replay_path;  ///< empty if no dump directory was given
};
class FlightRecorder {
public:
    FlightRecorder(std::string manifest_text, std::uint64_t seed, std::uint32_t threads)
        : manifest_(std::move(manifest_text)), seed_(seed), threads_(threads) {}
    /// Marks a pipeline stage as started (bounded history of 64 stages).
    void stage(std::string_view name) {
        if (stages_.size() == 64) stages_.erase(stages_.begin());
        stages_.emplace_back(name);
    }
    using Centroids = std::span<const std::array<double, 3>>;
    /// Invariant: no face may be FaceClass::INVALID.
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Violation> check_face_classes(std::span<const FaceClass> classes, Centroids c) const {
        std::vector<Violation> v;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < classes.size(); ++i) {
            if (classes[i] == FaceClass::INVALID) v.push_back({ViolationKind::InvalidFaceClass, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i), 255.0, at(c, i)});
        }
        return v;
    }
    /// Invariant: AMIPS <= 1e4 for every cell (NaN counts as a violation).
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Violation> check_amips(std::span<const double> energies, Centroids c) const {
        std::vector<Violation> v;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < energies.size(); ++i) {
            if (!(energies[i] <= kAmipsLimit)) v.push_back({ViolationKind::AmipsExceeded, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(i), energies[i], at(c, i)});
        }
        return v;
    }
    /// Invariant: n_nan_cells == 0 (infinities count as well).
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<Violation> check_nan(std::span<const double> field, Centroids c) const {
        std::vector<Violation> v;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < field.size(); ++i) {
