import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Btn, Chip, Code, Lead, Panel, Stat, sci } from "@/components/ui";
import type { Tone } from "@/components/ui";
import type { ProjectResult } from "@/wasm/core";
import { cn } from "@/utils/cn";
interface Edit {
  label: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
}
const EDIT_GROUPS: { title: string; tone: Tone; expect: string; edits: Edit[] }[] = [
  {
    title: "Cosmetic",
    tone: "ok",
    expect: "no key changes",
    edits: [
      { label: "Add a review comment", from: "[solver]\n", to: "# reviewed by QA, ticket CAE-1182\n[solver]\n" },
      { label: "Respell a number: 50.0 → 50", from: "cfl = 50.0", to: "cfl = 50" },
      { label: "solver.threads 8 → 64", from: "threads = 8 ", to: "threads = 64" },
    ],
  },
  {
    title: "Physics / solver",
    tone: "info",
    expect: "mesh re-used, re-solve",
    edits: [
      { label: "max_iterations 2000 → 2500", from: "max_iterations = 2000", to: "max_iterations = 2500" },
      { label: "Inlet pressure 180 → 190 kPa", from: "absolute_pa = 180000.0", to: "absolute_pa = 190000.0" },
      { label: "Solid conductivity 11.4 → 12.0", from: "k_w_m_k = 11.4", to: "k_w_m_k = 12.0" },
    ],
  },
  {
    title: "Meshing intent",
    tone: "warn",
    expect: "re-mesh",
    edits: [
      { label: "Prism layers 12 → 14", from: "layers = 12", to: "layers = 14" },
      { label: "Wake refinement 0.5 → 0.4 mm", from: "size_m = 5.0e-4", to: "size_m = 4.0e-4" },
      { label: "Tighten blade selector", from: "abs(nz) < 0.8", to: "abs(nz) < 0.7" },
    ],
  },
  {
    title: "Geometry",
    tone: "bad",
    expect: "everything stale",
    edits: [{ label: "New CAD revision (sha256)", from: "9f2c6f0d", to: "a81b03c7" }],
  },
];
const INVALID_EDITS: Edit[] = [
  { label: "Typo: clf = 5.0", from: "cfl = 50.0", to: "cfl = 50.0\nclf = 5.0" },
  { label: "Gauge pressure at a compressible inlet", from: "pressure = { absolute_pa = 180000.0 }", to: "pressure = { gauge_pa = 78675.0 }" },
  { label: "Bare pressure number", from: "pressure = { absolute_pa = 180000.0 }", to: "pressure = 180000.0" },
  { label: "y+ target without L_ref", from: ", l_ref_m = 0.08 }", to: " }" },
  { label: 'length_unit = "parsec"', from: 'length_unit = "mm"', to: 'length_unit = "parsec"' },
];
interface Props {
  manifest: string;
  setManifest: (s: string) => void;
  baseline: string;
  setBaseline: (s: string) => void;
  example: string;
  result: ProjectResult;
}
export function ProjectTab({ manifest, setManifest, baseline, setBaseline, example, result }: Props) {
  const diff = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const l of baseline.split("\n")) counts.set(l, (counts.get(l) ?? 0) + 1);
    let added = 0;
    for (const l of manifest.split("\n")) {
      const n = counts.get(l) ?? 0;
      if (n > 0) counts.set(l, n - 1);
      else added++;
    }
    let removed = 0;
    for (const n of counts.values()) removed += n;
    return { added, removed };
  }, [manifest, baseline]);
  const byteLen = useMemo(() => new TextEncoder().encode(manifest).length, [manifest]);
  const limit = 10240;
  const base = result.ok && result.baseline && result.baseline.ok ? result.baseline : null;
  const stages = result.ok
    ? [
        { name: "Tessellation", sub: "[geometry]", key: result.keys.geometry, file: "", changed: base?.geometry_changed ?? false },
        {
          name: "Volume mesh",
          sub: "geometry + [mesh] + boundary selectors",
          key: result.keys.mesh,
          file: result.mesh_file,
          changed: base?.mesh_changed ?? false,
        },
        {
          name: "Solution",
          sub: "mesh + materials + physics + solver − threads",
          key: result.keys.physics,
          file: result.solution_file,
          changed: base?.physics_changed ?? false,
        },
      ]
    : [];
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Lead>
        <code className="text-slate-200">project.toml</code> holds design intent only and stays under 10 KiB. Everything derived lives in{" "}
        <code className="text-slate-200">cache/*.mphys</code>, addressed by SHA-256 hashes of the manifest sub-trees that actually influence
        it. Edit the file or apply an edit below: the Rust core re-parses, re-validates and re-hashes on every keystroke.
      </Lead>
      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.05fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <Panel
          kicker="authoritative intent"
          title="project.toml"
          right={
            <>
              <Chip tone={byteLen > limit ? "bad" : "muted"}>
                {byteLen} / {limit} B
              </Chip>
              <Chip tone={diff.added + diff.removed === 0 ? "muted" : "info"}>
                +{diff.added} −{diff.removed} vs HEAD
              </Chip>
              <Btn onClick={() => setBaseline(manifest)} disabled={!result.ok || manifest === baseline} title="Use the current text as the committed baseline">
                Commit as HEAD
              </Btn>
              <Btn
                onClick={() => {
                  setManifest(example);
                  setBaseline(example);
                }}
              >
                Reset
              </Btn>
            </>
          }
          bodyClassName="p-0"
        >
          <div className="h-1 bg-slate-800">
            <div
              className={cn("h-full", byteLen > limit ? "bg-rose-400" : "bg-amber-400/70")}
              style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (byteLen / limit) * 100)}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="manifest-editor">
            project.toml contents
          </label>
          <textarea
            id="manifest-editor"
            value={manifest}
            onChange={(e) => setManifest(e.target.value)}
            spellCheck={false}
            autoCapitalize="off"
            autoCorrect="off"
            wrap="off"
            className="block h-[560px] w-full resize-y bg-[#0a0e13] p-4 text-[12px] leading-[1.6] text-slate-200 focus:outline-none"
          />
          {!result.ok && (
            <div role="alert" className="border-t border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-rose-200">
              <p className="font-mono text-[10px] tracking-widest text-rose-300 uppercase">
                rejected{result.line ? ` · line ${result.line}, column ${result.col}` : ""}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 font-mono break-words">{result.error}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <Panel
            kicker="content-addressed cache"
            title="What must be regenerated?"
            right={result.ok && <Chip tone={base && (base.mesh_changed || base.geometry_changed) ? "warn" : "ok"}>{base ? base.decision.split(":")[0] : "baseline"}</Chip>}
          >
            {result.ok ? (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {stages.map((s) => (
                  <div key={s.name} className={cn("rounded border px-3 py-2", s.changed ? "border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/5" : "border-emerald-500/25 bg-emerald-500/5")}>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <p className="text-[13px] font-medium text-slate-100">
                        {s.name} <span className="ml-1 text-[11px] font-normal text-slate-500">{s.sub}</span>
                      </p>
                      <Chip tone={s.changed ? "warn" : "ok"}>{s.changed ? "REGENERATE" : s.file ? "REUSE · mmap zero-copy" : "REUSE"}</Chip>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1 truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400" title={s.key}>
                      sha256 {s.key.slice(0, 32)}…
                    </p>
                    {s.file && <p className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500">{s.file}</p>}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {base && <p className="pt-1 text-[12px] text-slate-400">{base.decision}.</p>}
                <p className="text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
                  Hashes are taken over the parsed document, so comments, whitespace, key order and <code>50</code> vs <code>50.0</code> never
                  invalidate anything. <code>solver.threads</code> is excluded because results are bit-identical on any thread count.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[12.5px] text-slate-400">
                The manifest does not validate, so no keys are derived. Nothing is meshed or solved from an invalid file.
              </p>
            )}
          </Panel>
          <Panel kicker="try it" title="Apply an edit">
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {EDIT_GROUPS.map((g) => (
                <div key={g.title}>
                  <p className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    {g.title} <Chip tone={g.tone}>{g.expect}</Chip>
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {g.edits.map((e) => (
                      <Btn key={e.label} disabled={!manifest.includes(e.from)} onClick={() => setManifest(manifest.replace(e.from, e.to))}>
                        {e.label}
                      </Btn>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              <div>
                <p className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  Mistakes <Chip tone="bad">rejected with a path</Chip>
                </p>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {INVALID_EDITS.map((e) => (
                    <Btn key={e.label} disabled={!manifest.includes(e.from)} onClick={() => setManifest(manifest.replace(e.from, e.to))}>
                      {e.label}
                    </Btn>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Panel>
          {result.ok && (
            <Panel kicker="typed view" title={result.name}>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <Stat label="CAD source" value={result.geometry.source.split("/").pop()} sub={`sha256 ${result.geometry.sha256.slice(0, 12)}…`} />
                <Stat label="Source unit" value={result.geometry.length_unit} sub={`× ${result.geometry.metres_per_unit} → m, once`} />
                <Stat label="Base / min size" value={`${sci(result.mesh.base_size_m * 1e3)} / ${sci(result.mesh.min_size_m * 1e3)} mm`} sub={`growth ${result.mesh.growth_rate}`} />
                {result.mesh.prism && (
                  <Stat
                    label="First prism layer"
                    value={result.mesh.prism.first_layer_m ? `${sci(result.mesh.prism.first_layer_m * 1e6)} µm` : "error"}
                    sub={
                      result.mesh.prism.spec === "target_y_plus"
                        ? `from y+ = ${result.mesh.prism.target_y_plus}, Re = ${sci(result.mesh.prism.reynolds ?? 0, 2)}`
                        : "thickness_m given directly"
                    }
                  />
                )}
                <Stat label="Boundaries" value={result.boundaries.length} sub="bound by selectors" />
                <Stat label="Solver" value={`${result.solver.max_iterations} it`} sub={`seed ${result.solver.seed} · ${result.solver.threads} threads`} />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {result.modules.map((m) => (
                  <Chip key={m} tone="info">
                    {m}
                  </Chip>
                ))}
                {result.materials.map((m) => (
                  <Chip key={m.id}>
                    {m.id}: {m.kind}
                  </Chip>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Panel>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Panel kicker="on disk" title="Repository layout">
        <Code>{`my_case/
├── project.toml                         # tracked in Git, reviewed in pull requests
├── cad/vane_passage.step                # referenced by path + SHA-256 (Git LFS or artefact store)
└── cache/                               # .gitignore'd, safe to delete, rebuilt on demand
    ├── ${result.ok ? result.mesh_file.replace("cache/", "") : "mesh_<mesh key>.mphys"}
    └── ${result.ok ? result.solution_file.replace("cache/", "") : "solution_<physics key>.mphys"}
.mphys = 64-byte header | section table | 64-byte aligned payloads (f64 / u32 / bytes), per-section checksum
         opened read-only with mmap; arrays are borrowed straight from the page cache`}</Code>
      </Panel>
    </div>
  );
}

Created src/tabs/SelectorsTab.tsx
Created src/tabs/SelectorsTab.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Btn, Chip, Lead, Panel, sci } from "@/components/ui";
import { Viewer3D } from "@/components/Viewer3D";
import type { ViewFace } from "@/components/Viewer3D";
import { RULE_COLORS, SCENARIOS, buildScene, setBoundarySelect, toTsv } from "@/lib/scene";
import type { ScenarioId } from "@/lib/scene";
import { OP, SEP } from "@/wasm/core";
import type { BindResult, Core, ProjectOk, SelectResult } from "@/wasm/core";
import { cn } from "@/utils/cn";
interface Props {
  core: Core;
  manifest: string;
  setManifest: (s: string) => void;
  /** Last manifest that validated; keeps the rule list editable while a selector is mid-edit. */
  lastGood: ProjectOk | null;
  manifestOk: boolean;
}
const KIND_LABEL = { zero_match: "ZERO MATCH", overlap: "OVERLAP", unbound: "UNBOUND" } as const;
const vec = (v: number[]) => `(${v.map((x) => sci(x, 2)).join(", ")})`;
export function SelectorsTab({ core, manifest, setManifest, lastGood, manifestOk }: Props) {
  const [scenario, setScenario] = useState<ScenarioId>("baseline");
  const [hoverIds, setHoverIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [hoverFace, setHoverFace] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('cad_name ~= "blade_ss_*" and nx < 0');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (manifestOk) setDrafts({});
  }, [manifestOk, manifest]);
  const groups = useMemo(() => buildScene(scenario), [scenario]);
  const baseGroups = useMemo(() => buildScene("baseline"), []);
  const tsv = useMemo(() => toTsv(groups), [groups]);
  const baseTsv = useMemo(() => toTsv(baseGroups), [baseGroups]);
  const bind = useMemo(() => core.json<BindResult>(OP.BIND, manifest + SEP + tsv + SEP + baseTsv), [core, manifest, tsv, baseTsv]);
  const baseBind = useMemo(() => core.json<BindResult>(OP.BIND, manifest + SEP + baseTsv + SEP), [core, manifest, baseTsv]);
  const adhoc = useMemo(() => core.json<SelectResult>(OP.SELECT, query + SEP + tsv), [core, query, tsv]);
  const sc = SCENARIOS.find((s) => s.id === scenario)!;
  const ruleOf = new Map<number, number>();
  const alertIds = new Set<number>();
  if (bind.ok) {
    for (const a of bind.assignment) ruleOf.set(a.id, a.rule);
    for (const c of bind.conflicts) for (const f of c.faces) alertIds.add(f.id);
  }
  const adhocIds = new Set(adhoc.ok ? adhoc.ids : []);
  const hoverSet = new Set(hoverIds);
  const faces: ViewFace[] = groups.flatMap((g) =>
    g.polygons.map((points, i) => {
      const r = ruleOf.get(g.id) ?? -1;
      return {
        key: `${g.id}:${i}`,
        points,
        normal: g.normal,
        color: r >= 0 ? RULE_COLORS[r % RULE_COLORS.length] : "#64748b",
        alert: alertIds.has(g.id),
        hover: hoverSet.has(g.id) || hoverFace === g.id || (hoverIds.length === 0 && hoverFace === null && adhocIds.has(g.id)),
        title: `#${g.id} ${g.cadName}`,
      };
    }),
  );
  // What a legacy "list of face ids" binding recorded on revision A would point at now.
  const legacy = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Map(groups.map((g) => [g.id, g.cadName]));
    const total = baseGroups.length;
    const valid = baseGroups.filter((g) => now.get(g.id) === g.cadName).length;
    return { total, valid };
  }, [groups, baseGroups]);
  const rules = lastGood?.boundaries ?? [];
  const info = hoverFace !== null ? groups.find((g) => g.id === hoverFace) : null;
  const baselineClean = baseBind.ok && baseBind.bound;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Lead>
        Boundary conditions are bound by predicates over face groups, never by face ids. After a CAD edit the same rules are evaluated again.
        If a rule matches nothing, or two rules claim the same face, the core refuses to guess and returns a{" "}
        <code className="text-slate-200">TopologyRemapConflict</code> with the offending face-group ids and centroids — the faces pulsing red
        below.
      </Lead>
      <Panel kicker="simulate a CAD revision" title="Geometry edit">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {SCENARIOS.map((s) => (
            <Btn key={s.id} variant={s.id === scenario ? "active" : "ghost"} onClick={() => setScenario(s.id)} aria-pressed={s.id === scenario}>
              {s.title}
            </Btn>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] text-slate-400">{sc.edit}</p>
      </Panel>
      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <Panel
            kicker="vane passage · 0.30 × 0.12 × 0.10 m"
            title="Face groups coloured by bound rule"
            right={
              bind.ok ? (
                <Chip tone={bind.bound ? "ok" : "bad"}>{bind.bound ? "BOUND" : `${bind.conflicts.length} CONFLICT${bind.conflicts.length > 1 ? "S" : ""}`}</Chip>
              ) : (
                <Chip tone="bad">MANIFEST INVALID</Chip>
              )
            }
          >
            <Viewer3D
              faces={faces}
              label="3D view of the vane passage face groups"
              onHover={(k) => setHoverFace(k ? Number(k.split(":")[0]) : null)}
            />
            <p className="mt-2 min-h-[2.4em] font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
              {info
                ? `#${info.id} ${info.cadName} · normal ${vec(info.normal)} · centroid ${vec(info.centroid)} · area ${sci(info.area)} m²`
                : "Drag to rotate. Near walls are drawn as outlines so the blade stays visible. Hover a face for its geometric summary."}
            </p>
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
              {groups.map((g) => {
                const r = ruleOf.get(g.id) ?? -1;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={g.id}
                    type="button"
                    onPointerEnter={() => setHoverFace(g.id)}
                    onPointerLeave={() => setHoverFace(null)}
                    onFocus={() => setHoverFace(g.id)}
                    onBlur={() => setHoverFace(null)}
                    className={cn(
                      "rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10.5px]",
                      alertIds.has(g.id) ? "border-rose-400 text-rose-200" : "border-slate-700 text-slate-300",
                    )}
                    style={{ borderLeftWidth: 4, borderLeftColor: r >= 0 ? RULE_COLORS[r % RULE_COLORS.length] : "#64748b" }}
                  >
                    {g.id} {g.cadName}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </Panel>
          <Panel kicker="for comparison" title="What a face-id list would do">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <Chip tone={legacy.valid === legacy.total ? "ok" : "bad"}>
                {legacy.valid} / {legacy.total} recorded ids still name the same face
              </Chip>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-slate-400">
                {legacy.valid === legacy.total
                  ? "On an unchanged topology an id list survives. Any split, merge or re-export renumbers it."
                  : legacy.valid === 0
                    ? "Every id recorded on revision A now points at a different face or at nothing; boundary conditions would be lost or, worse, silently moved."
                    : "Some recorded ids now point at a different face or at nothing."}
              </p>
            </div>
          </Panel>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <Panel kicker="[[boundary]] in project.toml" title="Rules" right={!baselineClean && manifestOk ? <Chip tone="warn">revision A no longer binds cleanly</Chip> : null}>
            {!manifestOk && (
              <p role="alert" className="mb-3 rounded border border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-500/10 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-words text-rose-200">
                {bind.ok ? "" : bind.error}
              </p>
            )}
            <ul className="space-y-2">
              {rules.map((r, i) => {
                const matched = bind.ok ? (bind.rules[i]?.groups ?? []) : [];
                return (
                  <li
                    key={r.name}
                    onPointerEnter={() => setHoverIds(matched)}
                    onPointerLeave={() => setHoverIds([])}
                    className="rounded border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 p-2.5"
                    style={{ borderLeftWidth: 4, borderLeftColor: RULE_COLORS[i % RULE_COLORS.length] }}
                  >
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <p className="text-[12.5px] font-medium text-slate-100">
                        {r.name} <span className="ml-1 font-mono text-[10.5px] font-normal text-slate-500">{r.type}</span>
                      </p>
                      <Chip tone={!bind.ok ? "muted" : matched.length === 0 ? "bad" : "ok"}>
                        {bind.ok ? `${matched.length} face group${matched.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : "—"}
                      </Chip>
                    </div>
                    <label className="sr-only" htmlFor={`sel-${r.name}`}>
                      selector for {r.name}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id={`sel-${r.name}`}
                      value={drafts[r.name] ?? r.select}
                      spellCheck={false}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        setDrafts((d) => ({ ...d, [r.name]: e.target.value }));
                        setManifest(setBoundarySelect(manifest, r.name, e.target.value));
                      }}
                      className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded border border-slate-700 bg-[#0a0e13] px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[11.5px] text-slate-500">
              Editing a selector edits <code>project.toml</code>; because selectors shape prism layers they are part of the mesh key.
            </p>
          </Panel>
          <Panel
            kicker="strict ambiguity contract"
            title="TopologyRemapConflict"
            right={bind.ok && <Chip tone={bind.conflicts.length ? "bad" : "ok"}>{bind.conflicts.length ? "binding refused" : "none"}</Chip>}
          >
            {bind.ok && bind.conflicts.length === 0 && (
              <p className="text-[12.5px] text-emerald-300">
                All {groups.length} face groups are claimed by exactly one of {bind.rules.length} rules. No clicks, no ids.
              </p>
            )}
            {bind.ok && bind.conflicts.length > 0 && (
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {bind.conflicts.map((c, i) => (
                  <li
                    key={i}
                    onPointerEnter={() => setHoverIds(c.faces.map((f) => f.id))}
                    onPointerLeave={() => setHoverIds([])}
                    className="rounded border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/5 p-2.5"
                  >
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <Chip tone="bad">{KIND_LABEL[c.kind]}</Chip>
                      <span className="font-mono text-[12px] text-slate-200">
                        {c.rule || "(no rule)"}
                        {c.other_rule ? ` ∩ ${c.other_rule}` : ""}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-slate-300">{c.message}</p>
                    <table className="mt-2 w-full font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                      <tbody>
                        {c.faces.map((f) => (
                          <tr key={f.id} className="border-t border-slate-800/80">
                            <td className="py-1 pr-2 text-rose-200">#{f.id}</td>
                            <td className="py-1 pr-2 text-slate-300">{f.cad_name}</td>
                            <td className="py-1 text-right">centroid {vec(f.centroid)}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                        {c.faces.length === 0 && (
                          <tr>
                            <td className="py-1">no candidate face groups</td>
                          </tr>
                        )}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
            {bind.ok && bind.conflicts.length > 0 && sc.fix && (
              <div className="mt-3 rounded border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5 p-2.5">
                <p className="text-[12px] text-slate-300">
                  <span className="font-medium text-amber-200">A deliberate fix:</span> {sc.fix.why}
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] break-words text-slate-400">
                  {sc.fix.boundary}.select = {sc.fix.select}
                </p>
                <Btn className="mt-2" variant="solid" onClick={() => setManifest(setBoundarySelect(manifest, sc.fix!.boundary, sc.fix!.select))}>
                  Apply to project.toml
                </Btn>
              </div>
            )}
          </Panel>
          <Panel kicker="scratch pad" title="Try a selector">
            <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="adhoc">
              ad-hoc selector
            </label>
            <input
              id="adhoc"
              value={query}
              spellCheck={false}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded border border-slate-700 bg-[#0a0e13] px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-[12px] text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 focus:outline-none"
            />
            {adhoc.ok ? (
              <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-slate-400">
                <span className="text-emerald-300">{adhoc.ids.length} match</span> · ids [{adhoc.ids.join(", ")}] · {adhoc.ops} instructions, stack
                depth {adhoc.stack}, zero heap allocations per evaluation
              </p>
            ) : (
              <pre className="mt-2 overflow-x-auto font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-snug text-rose-300">
                {query}
                {"\n"}
                {" ".repeat(Math.min(adhoc.pos ?? 0, query.length))}^ {adhoc.error}
              </pre>
            )}
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {[
                "normal.dot([-1,0,0]) > 0.995 and abs(x - bbox.x_min) < 1e-4",
                "in_cylinder([0.15,0,-0.01],[0.15,0,0.11],0.05) and curvature > 10",
                "abs(nz) > 0.995 and area > 0.005",
                "sdf_box([0.09,-0.03,0],[0.21,0.03,0.1]) < 0 and not (cad_name ~= \"blade_*\")",
                "angle_deg(normal, [0,0,-1]) < 50 and radius_yz < 0.03",
              ].map((q) => (
                <Btn key={q} className="font-mono text-[10.5px]" onClick={() => setQuery(q)}>
                  {q}
                </Btn>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Panel>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Created src/tabs/UnitsTab.tsx
Created src/tabs/UnitsTab.tsx

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Chip, Code, Field, Lead, Panel, Stat, sci } from "@/components/ui";
import { COMPILE_FAIL_LOG } from "@/lib/sources";
import { OP } from "@/wasm/core";
import type { Core, UnitsResult } from "@/wasm/core";
const BASE = ["kg", "m", "s", "K", "mol", "A", "cd"];
const KIND = ["plain", "absolute pressure", "gauge pressure", "p_rgh", "first-layer thickness", "target y+"];
export function UnitsTab({ core }: { core: Core }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState({
    yplus: 1,
    u: 120,
    rho: 1.18,
    mu: 1.8e-5,
    l: 0.08,
    pAbs: 101325,
    t: 288.15,
    r: 287.05,
    pGauge: -2000,
    pRef: 101325,
  });
  const set = (k: keyof typeof v) => (x: number) => setV((old) => ({ ...old, [k]: x }));
  const res = useMemo(
    () => core.json<UnitsResult>(OP.UNITS, [v.yplus, v.u, v.rho, v.mu, v.l, v.pAbs, v.t, v.r, v.pGauge, v.pRef].join(" ")),
    [core, v],
  );
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Lead>
        Every physical value is a <code className="text-slate-200">Quantity</code> whose seven SI exponents live in the type. Products and
        quotients are derived by the compiler; <code className="text-slate-200">Length + Pressure</code> does not build. Quantities that share
        a dimension but not a meaning — absolute vs gauge pressure, a first-layer thickness vs a y⁺ target — are separate types as well.
      </Lead>
      {!res.ok ? (
        <Panel title="Inputs incomplete">
          <p className="text-[12.5px] text-rose-300">{res.error}</p>
        </Panel>
      ) : null}
      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <Panel kicker="TargetYPlus → FirstLayerThicknessM" title="A y⁺ target is not a length">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-5">
            <Field label="target y⁺" unit="–" value={v.yplus} onChange={set("yplus")} />
            <Field label="U∞" unit="m/s" value={v.u} onChange={set("u")} />
            <Field label="ρ" unit="kg/m³" value={v.rho} onChange={set("rho")} />
            <Field label="μ" unit="Pa·s" value={v.mu} onChange={set("mu")} />
            <Field label="L_ref" unit="m" value={v.l} onChange={set("l")} />
          </div>
          {res.ok && res.yplus.ok ? (
            <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
              <Stat label="Re_L" value={sci(res.yplus.reynolds)} sub="ρ U L / μ : dimensionless" />
              <Stat label="C_f" value={sci(res.yplus.cf)} sub="0.058 Re^-0.2" />
              <Stat label="u_τ" value={`${sci(res.yplus.u_tau)} m/s`} sub={`τ_w = ${sci(res.yplus.tau_w)} Pa`} />
              <Stat label="Δy₁ for the mesher" value={`${sci(res.yplus.first_layer_m * 1e6)} µm`} sub="FirstLayerThicknessM" tone="ok" />
            </div>
          ) : (
            res.ok && !res.yplus.ok && <p className="mt-3 font-mono text-[12px] text-rose-300">{res.yplus.error}</p>
          )}
          <p className="mt-3 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
            The mesher only accepts <code>FirstLayerThicknessM</code>. A <code>TargetYPlus</code> can become one solely through{" "}
            <code>resolve(&amp;FlowReference)</code>, so the reference state (U∞, ρ, μ, L_ref) can never be forgotten. Flat-plate estimate; the
            wall spacing convention is Δy₁ = y⁺ μ / (ρ u_τ).
          </p>
        </Panel>
        <Panel kicker="AbsolutePressurePa · GaugePressurePa · ReducedPressurePRgh" title="Pressures that cannot be mixed up">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <Field label="p absolute" unit="Pa" value={v.pAbs} onChange={set("pAbs")} />
            <Field label="T" unit="K" value={v.t} onChange={set("t")} />
            <Field label="R specific" unit="J/(kg·K)" value={v.r} onChange={set("r")} />
          </div>
          {res.ok && (
            <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[12px]">
              {res.ideal_gas.ok ? (
                <span className="text-emerald-300">ideal_gas_density(AbsolutePressurePa, R, T) = {sci(res.ideal_gas.rho, 4)} kg/m³</span>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-rose-300">AbsolutePressurePa::new rejected the value: {res.ideal_gas.error}</span>
              )}
            </p>
          )}
          <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <Field label="p gauge" unit="Pa" value={v.pGauge} onChange={set("pGauge")} />
            <Field label="reference (absolute)" unit="Pa" value={v.pRef} onChange={set("pRef")} />
          </div>
          {res.ok && (
            <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 font-mono text-[12px]">
              {res.gauge_to_absolute.ok ? (
                <p className="text-emerald-300">gauge.to_absolute(reference) = {sci(res.gauge_to_absolute.absolute_pa, 6)} Pa</p>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-rose-300">gauge.to_absolute(reference) → {res.gauge_to_absolute.error}</p>
              )}
              <p className="text-slate-400">
                decoding the gauge wire record as absolute → <span className="text-amber-300">{res.gauge_wire_as_absolute}</span>
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
          <p className="mt-3 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
            Try a negative absolute pressure, or a gauge pressure below −reference. The ideal-gas law has no overload for gauge or p_rgh
            values; the only conversion path names its reference explicitly.
          </p>
        </Panel>
      </div>
      {res.ok && (
        <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
          <Panel kicker="exponents read back from the inferred result types" title="Dimension algebra done by the compiler">
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[520px] text-left font-mono text-[11.5px]">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="text-slate-500">
                    <th className="py-1 pr-3 font-normal">expression</th>
                    <th className="py-1 pr-3 font-normal">result type</th>
                    {BASE.map((b) => (
                      <th key={b} className="w-7 py-1 text-center font-normal">
                        {b}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {res.derivations.map((d) => (
                    <tr key={d.expr} className="border-t border-slate-800">
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-3 text-slate-300">{d.expr}</td>
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-3 text-amber-200">{d.name}</td>
                      {d.dim.map((e, i) => (
                        <td key={i} className={`py-1.5 text-center ${e === 0 ? "text-slate-700" : e > 0 ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-sky-300"}`}>
                          {e}
                        </td>
                      ))}
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </Panel>
          <Panel kicker="16 bytes · little-endian · identical in Rust and C++" title="7-element SI wire contract">
            <p className="mb-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              <span className="text-sky-300">f64 value</span> · <span className="text-emerald-300">i8 × 7 exponents (kg m s K mol A cd)</span> ·{" "}
              <span className="text-amber-300">u8 kind</span>
            </p>
            <ul className="space-y-1.5">
              {res.wire.map((w) => (
                <li key={w.name} className="rounded border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[12px] text-slate-200">{w.name}</span>
                    <Chip>{KIND[w.kind] ?? w.kind}</Chip>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-all">
                    <span className="text-sky-300">{w.hex.slice(0, 16)}</span> <span className="text-emerald-300">{w.hex.slice(16, 30)}</span>{" "}
                    <span className="text-amber-300">{w.hex.slice(30)}</span>
                  </p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[11.5px] text-slate-500">
              A receiver checks exponents and kind before accepting a value, so a unit or meaning mismatch between processes is an error
              instead of a wrong number.
            </p>
          </Panel>
        </div>
      )}
      <Panel kicker="core/logs/compile-fail.txt" title="Captured compiler output for code that must not build">
        <Code className="max-h-[420px]">{COMPILE_FAIL_LOG}</Code>
      </Panel>
    </div>
  );
}
